**Items 1/4, shared activity presentation:** One shared step-mode chart/activity component for own simulation, Sarah and Daniel, no per-example fork. Stack follows the plot in the shared chart region, one card per deposit/withdrawal, newest first, newest initially selected. Sarah's fee card and Daniel's disclosures/invariant stay. Daniel's two earlier reading rows stay in the list; remove only the terminal `9 Oct · shown above` row. No reading becomes a simulated-change card. Exactly one clay accent on the chart follows the selected card; no extra clay bullet on the card. No highlight in returning no-change state; no chart rule or `Your last visit` label on any of these three surfaces. Zero-change has no rule, eyebrow, clay highlight, `0 changes` counter or extra line. Preserve `No deposits yet. Your first one will start this chart.` and the existing no-change status. The oldest first-deposit card shows its amount, expressly superseding sole-first-deposit hero deferral for that card. No totals, gain/loss colour, position count, pager, auto-advance or loop. No value appears on both chart and card. The native list is an alternative disclosure, collapsed by default, without duplicate simultaneous spoken event detail or authored count.

**Locked activity copy / currency:** Card grammar is `{Type} · {d MMM} · {sign}{recorded amount}`, Type `Withdrawal`, `Deposit`, or `First deposit` for oldest first deposit; signs `−` (U+2212) / `+`. USD examples `Withdrawal · 9 Oct · −$20.00`, `Deposit · 5 Oct · +$200.00`, `First deposit · {d MMM} · +$X` illustrate USD entries only. Amounts use existing shared currency formatters in the recorded event currency, with no conversion. Sarah's 14 April event is ZMW 4,000.00 (`First deposit` as the oldest card); later deposit ZMW 2,000.00. Own-sim Home event amounts are ledger amount_usd, mapped without conversion to USD; Daniel registry events are USD; Sarah registry events are ZMW. Supported current Home entry-currency set: USD/ZMW. Source-local input metadata on own-sim deposits is not a new card conversion or a replacement for its recorded USD event amount. Hero preference changes neither visible card nor SR currency/amount. Registry examples stay fixed under all five hero preferences; HomeHero, shared hero formatter/rates and all financial facts stay unchanged. No missing/unsupported event currency or amount may be guessed or derived; stop with evidence.

- Hint: `Swipe to see earlier changes`, touch only; hidden for reduced motion, keyboard/desktop and when no earlier card exists. Disappears after the first successful change or key press for that mount; no persistent flag or end hint.
- Visible controls and identical accessible names: `Earlier change` / `Later change`, disabled at bounded ends. Keyboard/non-swipe access required; left/right keys and a polite status region may share the same selection without added copy. Reduced motion is an instant swap; no auto-advance.
- Native list title `All changes`, or `All changes · example dates` for Sarah/Daniel; collapsed by default; no written count. Preserve prior list button semantics where compatible with this native list.
- Eyebrow `Since your last visit` only for cards after last visit, hidden whenever the existing positive S2 summary is visible. Drop the SR since prefix in the same situations. Preserve existing summary wording/placement, including `1 change since your last visit`, `{n} changes since your last visit` and `No changes since your last visit`; no literal `1 changes` or added zero counter.
- SR after last visit: `Since your last visit. {Type}, {plus|minus} {amount} {currency name}, {d MMMM}.`; earlier cards: `{Type}, {plus|minus} {amount} {currency name}, {d MMMM}.`. Type matches the card, including `First deposit`. Example: `Since your last visit. Withdrawal, minus 20 US dollars, 9 October.` No symbol or `point highlighted` phrase. Prefix suppression follows the S2 rule above.
- Full currency names, singular only for exactly 1: `US dollar` / `US dollars`; `Zambian kwacha` / `Zambian kwacha`; `Nigerian naira` / `Nigerian naira`; `Kenyan shilling` / `Kenyan shillings`; `Ghanaian cedi` / `Ghanaian cedis`; `Philippine peso` / `Philippine pesos`. Implement the current USD/ZMW entry cases; retain the remaining locked names for later separately authorised entry support without adding such entries here. SR amount drops `.00` on whole amounts, otherwise two decimals, retains thousands separators, no minor-unit names.
- Visible dates use d MMM; SR full month. Retain UTC fictional/local own-sim boundaries, year appended after month only when different from current year. Preserve existing hero/example labels: `Your simulation` is own-sim only. Existing limits/disclosures stay where they are or directly under the stack within the existing component without added copy; no mockup date, axis label or balance is new product data.

**Item 2, NH3:** On `/withdraw?journey=class-a-val-002&scenario=unavailable-data` only, render the existing `Step 3 · check the position after a simulated withdrawal` header, exactly once `This example has no rate set, so the local amount and Confirm aren't available.`, and `Back to Home` as the only local action, pointing to journey Home. Remove the old panel, Retry rate, form and zero-balance deposit CTA in this scenario only. Global nav and study reachability stay. Scenario rate remains null for successful/loading/failing FX; no quote, Updated at or live/market-data implication. Deposit and ordinary Withdraw error/loading/zero-balance branches are unchanged.

**Item 3, receipt recovery:** Own-sim Deposit/Withdraw retain one sessionStorage presentation marker for the current action's identity and only the existing receipt view inputs required for restoration. No ledger schema/amount/arithmetic/settlement change or new financial data. Reuse existing transaction status and FIX-003 settlement recovery. After reload, pending stays pending until actual settlement, then show the existing confirmation receipt alone until acknowledgement through its existing next-action links using page-level capture. ActionDock and its labels remain byte-identical. Form returns on the next visit; no new dismiss copy. Same receipt and next actions as before; no create/append/confirm replay. Same-day recorded-time copy stays; a prior-day receipt uses `{d MMM}, HH:mm` through the narrow ReceiptCard formatter change. Ignore failed/malformed/stale/deleted/replaced/reset markers; no replay of old history lacking a marker. Storage-unavailable reload recovery is not claimed without persistent evidence. No CRF-003/multi-tab or FIX-004 change.

- E9 `protocol-line-10-exact`: line 10 equals D1; `supplies only the denomination` and `Record the selected currency` absent from the protocol.
- E10 `layout-a11y`: study entry at 1440, 390 and 320@200% has no horizontal overflow and passes axe; continue is keyboard reachable.

Existing affected tests: stability E2E ordinary/study-helper case, both R11 cases; reserve E2E flow, R11 and R16 cases use the entry helper. Amend all selector calls and return-selector assertions to seeded preference/absence/storage assertions, retaining all five currencies, research guards and ordinary-tab selection.

**Objective / locked decisions:** Both research pages present the same fictional examples as Home, using the existing registry and Home fixtures read-only, with one labelled what-if layered on top. Both pages share the ordered components and slots below; scope notes and authored labels are absent. The hypothetical provider accepts kwacha without any invented quote; no fee conversion. No monthly/planned contributions, K24,000 total, before/after panels, arrows or New markers. Figures appear once per page; the Hedgr stage contains only its locked paragraph and Home link. Both Daniel variants render the same page. Both pages are ZMW-only regardless of display preference. USD uses ungrouped `$1000.00` style with `toFixed(2)`, never `formatUsd`; kwacha delegates to existing `formatLocalCurrency` and uses `ZMW 6,000.00` style. Engine, Home, registry, orientation and bridge sources/output remain unchanged, subject only to the expressly accepted entry limitation below.

**Read-only fact sources:** `SYNTHETIC_JOURNEY_EXAMPLES` supplies identities, deposits and Sarah holding; `DANIEL_HOME_TIMELINE` / current Home read supplies Daniel holding and three dated local readings, with existing `DANIEL_HOME_TIMELINE_INPUTS` for numeric rate labels; `SARAH_HOME_READ` supplies U3 fee, as-of and due date. No registry/fixture/Engine/Home edit or source-of-truth move. Use computed minor-unit local amounts for the three Daniel readings, not parsed Engine display prose. Derive dates from existing ISO source values with explicit UTC formatting; no clock-dependent replacement dates. Frozen-registry sensitivity tests use a mocked replacement export/fresh import, never unfreeze or mutate production objects. Numeric fact literals are prohibited in research files; complete formatted money tokens are the say-once unit, not digit substrings in dates or rates.

**Locked user-facing copy:** Both pages use `ResearchChrome`, situation card (`rs.panel`), continue, what-if card, Hedgr stage (heading, paragraph, Home link), attribution, limits, next action, footer, in that order. Slots 3 and 15 are absent. The common boundary is `This example uses the facts on this page and nothing else. No real money is involved. Nothing you do here is saved.`

Orientation, selector implementation/output and stored preference behaviour remain frozen. **Acceptance criteria:**

1. Every slot string equals the table above; slot 3 absent on both pages.
2. Both pages render the same slot sequence through the same components.
3. All Daniel and Sarah figures and dates come from the registry and Home fixtures; no figure literal in research files.
4. USD `$1000.00` style, kwacha `ZMW 6,000.00` style; no `K\d`, no `USD 1,000`, no `formatUsd` import.
5. Each figure once per page; no before/after panels, `study-panel-before`/`after` gone, no arrows, no "New" markers.
6. Home links go to `?example=sarah` and `?example=daniel` and land on the matching Home example.
7. `?v=1` and `?v=2` render identical DOM.

Source-first technical closeout and final separate independently verified permanent-main rebind precede fetched-main RAP PASS. Close only this nested ticket; do not activate its successor. Rollback is one runtime revert; authority rollback uses governed source/rebind.

### Completed finite non-lane support — `OPS-GOV-CLEANUP-001` (closed §388 on source merge and final rebind; §386)

**Completion status (§388):** Implementation #890, independent exact-head PASS, successful hosted checks, post-implementation RAP and exact-revision Production check are recorded in §388. Closure takes effect after source closeout merge, final separate independently verified permanent-main RAP rebind (§364) and fetched-main RAP PASS. **Authority / class / start:** Founder Musalwa Hibajene, 14 Oct 2026 16:25 AWST in MonoCode via Dex; full word-for-word record in §386. Finite repository tooling/governance support, outside product/research lane occupancy; no D-number or Green delegation. No brief attachment or output artifact is committed.

**Exact implementation allowlist — eight paths:**
- `apps/frontend/tsconfig.lib.json` (new): extend existing strict app options; explicitly include all `lib/**/*.ts`, `lib/**/*.tsx`, ambient declarations and existing Next environment declarations, independent of app reachability. No emit; disable incremental or use a separate build-info path. Keep existing strictness and tests excluded.
- `apps/frontend/tsconfig.test-types.json` (new): extend existing strict app options, Vitest/Node types; explicitly check only `__tests__/dashboard.page.test.tsx`, `__tests__/home-small-fixes.test.tsx`, `__tests__/withdraw.page.test.tsx` plus existing ambient declarations; override inherited excludes so these three roots are really checked. This is a bounded check, not an all-tests coverage claim.
- `apps/frontend/package.json`: add named `typecheck:lib`, `typecheck:test-types` and coverage-check commands to the existing frontend `typecheck` chain. Existing root `pnpm run validate`, `.github/workflows/validate.yml` and `.github/workflows/ci.yml` already call workspace typecheck; use that wiring without changing workflows or required-check names.
- `scripts/ci/frontend-typecheck.test.mjs` (new): hermetic tooling regression checks for actual file coverage, command/CI wiring and compiler failure/success on deliberately broken/fixed unimported fixtures in disposable copies. No persistent runtime fixture or external calls; clean up only its own temporary files.
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/dashboard.page.test.tsx`: only type-annotate `makeBalanceState` return as `ReturnType<typeof useBalance>` (or equivalently narrow its existing currency literal). Preserve every assertion, value and behaviour.
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/home-small-fixes.test.tsx`: the same fixture-only type correction; preserve every assertion, value and behaviour.
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/withdraw.page.test.tsx`: replace only the two readonly `NODE_ENV` writes with typed `vi.stubEnv` calls using their existing exact values and existing restoration timing; no test flow, assertion or behaviour change. No new `unstubAllEnvs` lifecycle that changes other stubs.
- `docs/ops/runbook.md`: preserve the source-recorded exact-head hosted-E2E `run=` rule; bounded, evidence-qualified operational diagnosis/recovery for auto-merge stalls and Vercel Git-info failures. No weakening of verification/protection, token/secret introduction, new deployment authority, or unsupported root-cause claim.

All other paths remain unedited in implementation. Source stewardship separately changes only STATUS, AGENTS, runbook, the exact projected sequencing assertion in `apps/bridge-worker/tests/fork1-authority.test.mjs`, and generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json`; the permanent-main rebind changes only that generated file. No app runtime, lib runtime, workflow, root package, lockfile, existing tsconfig or external settings change is in this allowlist.

**Named failing-first checks / acceptance:**
1. `OPS lib typecheck includes unreachable Engine and lib modules`: enumerate actual checked files and compare to every production TypeScript file under `lib/engine/**` and `lib/**`; prove a new unimported Engine fixture and a non-Engine lib fixture containing a real assignment error make the proposed command fail, then pass after correcting only the disposable fixture. Red must fail for the intended coverage/diagnostic, not missing dependencies. No Home/lib runtime file is changed as a fix.

After the allowed types-only fixes the same check reports zero. Discovery corrects a baseline count, not scope: same three files and same two causes. Do not extend this to the 67 diagnostics found by checking all tests.
3. `OPS typecheck remains on validate and hosted CI paths`: mechanically check the new commands are called by frontend `typecheck`, root recursive `typecheck` and `validate`, and both existing hosted typecheck jobs. Existing application/Vitest checks remain in the chain. New coverage tests must actually run there.
4. `OPS test behaviour is preserved`: compare assertions/fixtures against immutable pre-ticket `fa78e22`, with only the named annotations and equivalent env writes allowed; run all three named Vitest suites and full validation. No assertion/value change, skip, suppression, cast-to-any workaround, lowered strictness, new dependency or blanket exclusion.
5. `OPS auto-merge recovery retains exact-head gates`: evidence index covers #868/#875 and #880/#885, current protection/rulesets and workflow authentication. If adding the smallest procedural recovery, require about five minutes of continued stall, same head/current independent PASS, all required checks green, up-to-date CLEAN state, one owner-account disable/re-enable of squash auto-merge, then observe GitHub merge. Never direct/admin merge. Exercise evidence predicates hermetically; do not claim the underlying GitHub fault is repaired.
6. `OPS Vercel diagnosis is evidence-bounded`: retain exact deployment IDs/SHAs, failure phase, empty logs and successful controls; compare Git/project metadata read-only. No speculative `vercel.json` fix. If no repo-file cause is established, report unresolved and stop for a separately confirmed external proposal rather than claiming success. No redeploy, reconnect, project change or protection change is authorised.
7. `OPS verifier run URL is exact-head hosted E2E`: runbook PASS/FAIL examples and explanatory rule identify the actual GitHub Actions E2E run URL for the reviewed PR head; verifier independently checks that run's head and success, then re-reads live head immediately before its sole attestation.

**Discovery evidence at immutable `fa78e22`:** Existing app config roots omit `lib/`; unreachable modules are not independently checked. Existing `tsconfig.vitest.json` inherits test excludes and lists zero test roots. Explicit strict discovery roots check 17 Engine/declaration files and 83 lib/declaration files with zero errors. The bounded three-test check finds 36 diagnostics; all-tests check finds 67 and is outside this repair. Frozen dependency install uses Node 20.19.5 / pnpm 9.12.0. No Engine or lib source errors surfaced; any later one is a stop, never permission to repair it.

**Auto-merge discovery / smallest proposal:** Live classic protection requires `validate`, `E2E smoke (@hedgr/frontend)` and `hedgr/verifier`, strict=true, admin enforcement, linear history and conversation resolution; auto-merge/squash enabled; no rulesets. #868 head `424d5ac` was disabled at 13:14:21Z, merged 13:14:38Z on 13 Oct; #875 head `4aa0360` was disabled 16:23:04Z, merged 16:23:08Z. #880/#885 have no disable event and merged unaided. The supplied CLEAN/green stall observations remain historical evidence, not a state reconstructable from merged PR metadata. The verifier workflow writes status using GITHUB_TOKEN on issue_comment/default-branch evaluation. GitHub's [GITHUB_TOKEN documentation](https://docs.github.com/en/actions/concepts/security/github_token) documents workflow-event suppression, not proof that auto-merge evaluation is suppressed. Do not assert a token root cause or replace it with a PAT/App. Smallest supported repo-file remedy is the bounded owner-account recovery procedure in check 5, preserving all §331 gates; root cause remains unresolved.

**Vercel discovery / proposed disposition:** No tracked Vercel config exists at baseline. API confirms preview `dpl_2NDERTw7vbtmznaSaw5CAcFCQ6EB` (#852, `932233c`) and `dpl_2aKnkWeZRTmRdVjufGNbJNyzZXTf` (#856, `d6b02cd`) both ERROR / `git_info_fail`, source=git, about four/five seconds after creation, with no build events. Later #868/#875/#880/#885 preview checks succeeded. This supports Git metadata acquisition failure, not a TypeScript/build error or a proved permissions/configuration defect. [Vercel Git documentation](https://vercel.com/docs/git) describes Git-triggered preview/Production deployment; it supplies no evidenced repository patch for this fault. No external-setting change is presently justified by the available evidence. Preserve the diagnostic, pursue only read-only evidence, and surface unresolved rather than invent a fix. Do not reconnect integrations or change project settings without the separate confirmation below.

**External-settings / other stop rules:** If any fix needs a GitHub repository/branch-protection/ruleset setting or Vercel project/dashboard setting change, do not change it: write the exact proposed change, evidence and trade-off and stop for Dex/Founder confirmation, without merging. Stop without merging on any Home (`apps/frontend/app/**`), Engine or other `lib/` runtime edit, behaviour change, weakened/skipped test, out-of-allowlist requirement, authority conflict or two independent Verifier FAILs. No contract impact implementation details may be decided and noted; contract-adjacent changes require Founder confirmation, contract changes stop. Unproven hypotheses are not approved external changes or completed fixes.

**Build / verification / closeout:** Docs/authority source is docs-first (§346); fixture/authority tests validate it. Implementer records failing-first tooling/test-type checks before the smallest green change; first implementation commit contains failing tests/checks only where applicable, then the types/config change, no product TDD or runtime scope inferred. Independent GPT-6.1 Sol high Verifier in isolated checkout confirms acceptance, reproduces red/green and current exact head, hosted `validate` and E2E success. Explicit brief permits exactly one runbook-format attestation with `run=` equal to that exact-head hosted GitHub Actions E2E run URL after immediate head re-read; Steward/Dex/Implementer never attest. §331 governed squash auto-merge only; owner-account branch updates invalidate PASS and require fresh independent verification. Record natural policy and harness limitations; no live provider/configuration change. Do not close the ticket while required fault resolution is unresolved without Founder disposition. Rollback tooling by single implementation revert; authority rollback through governed source/rebind. Only this support ticket can close; queue items need separate activation. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

### Completed nested Lane V — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-STABILITY-SARAH-CURRENCY-MATCH-001` (closed §383 on source merge and final rebind; Stability Engine; Engine-only; §382)

**Completion status (§383):** Runtime #880, exact-head independent PASS, unchanged post-runtime RAP, exact-revision READY Production and source/import/diff no-consumer/no-visible-change confirmation are recorded in §383. This retained brief is completed history after permanent-main source closeout, final separate independently verified permanent-main RAP rebind (§364) and fetched-main RAP PASS. No Production browser probe required or performed. No successor, consumer, continuing runtime or research-release authority follows. Historical activation wording below records §382 only.

**Authority / start:** The Founder assignment dated 14 Oct 2026 grants Repo Steward (§9.3) ACT_WITH_CONFIRMATION to record only this activation, after Ticket B’s §381 closeout/final rebind at fetched `origin/main` `61e6e6fd55d3909865a046cb26564abb56489d50`. It does not authorise this Steward to implement runtime. Founder Musalwa Hibajene (`mhibajene`), in MonoCode via Dex (§9.11), supplied the contract proposal and made the 13 Oct 2026 20:03 AWST D2/contract decision and 20:13 AWST copy lock recorded word for word in §382. Use the resolved contract below, not the proposal’s superseded C-USD/candidate strings or inferred uncertainty about §364. Sole nested Lane V under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; Stability Engine layer, Class A informational/synthetic, Green classification only, no standing delegation or D-number. `SE-REASON-001` stays open without a nested ticket; G deferred. Source merge → separate independently verified permanent-main RAP rebind (§364) → fetch main / `bridge:rap:check` PASS makes activation effective. Steward stops; Dex launches the separate GPT-6.1 Sol xhigh Implementer under §363.

**D2 currency-only carve-out — only `sarah-currency-match-v1`:** The Engine may state only the relationship between the currency Sarah’s savings are held in and the currency her fee is fixed in. The read takes no amounts and produces no coverage, gap, numerical comparison, progress, sufficiency, ability-to-pay or action output. No explanation names a savings or fee figure. This is an explicit module-and-version-limited exception to the §343 savings-relationship fence, not permission by analogy for any other version/module or savings read. Any read using savings amounts remains under the full §343 fence and needs its own Founder decision. Wherever a future consumer shows this explanation, `This read does not establish whether Sarah can pay the fee.` must remain visible next to it. That is a later presentation acceptance gate, not a new output field or consumer permission now. Existing sarah-read-v1 complete limits stay character-exact. Outside this carve-out, §343 remains fully operative; §347 language/non-execution and stability properties remain controlling.

**Runtime allowlist — exactly three new paths:**
- `apps/frontend/lib/engine/sarah-currency-match.ts` — pure separate read, exported function/types/version; no consumer.
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-sarah-currency-match.test.ts` — named failing-first Vitest contracts and type-level absence assertions.
- `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-sarah-currency-match.golden.ts` — exactly three independent literal explanation goldens, never imported by runtime.

Read-only use of `apps/frontend/lib/engine/sarah-read.ts` (`SARAH_EXAMPLE_INPUTS`, `computeSarahRead`, types/version) is permitted for alignment tests; no edit to it, its tests or goldens. No shared helper extraction or export change is authorised. Reproduce the existing validObservation semantics locally if needed; its private helper need not become a shared API. STATUS/AGENTS/authority fixture/generated RAP belong to source PRs, never this runtime allowlist. If another path is needed, stop and report before any change or merge.

**Exact input/output contract:** Export `SARAH_CURRENCY_MATCH_ENGINE_VERSION = 'sarah-currency-match-v1'` with its literal type and `computeSarahCurrencyMatch(input: SarahCurrencyMatchInput): SarahCurrencyMatch`. The caller cannot select the version. The exact types are:

```ts
type SarahCurrencyMatchInput = {
  savingsCurrency: 'ZMW';
  feeFixedCurrency: 'USD' | 'ZMW';
  priorFeeFixedCurrency?: 'USD' | 'ZMW';
  asOf?: string;
};

type SarahCurrencyMatch = {
  engineVersion: 'sarah-currency-match-v1';
  asOf?: string;
  savingsCurrency: 'ZMW';
  feeFixedCurrency: 'USD' | 'ZMW';
  relationship: 'different-currency' | 'same-currency';
  rateAffectsFeeLocalCost: boolean;
  framing: 'present-state' | 'changed-from-prior';
  explanation: string;
};
```

No amount, rate, display-currency or preference fields, index signature, tone, severity, status, limits field, prior-state output or extra output keys. Unknown input keys rejected. `asOf` is echoed exactly only when supplied and valid; when absent, omit the key entirely, with no clock default. Explicit present-but-invalid optional values are rejected, not treated as absence. `savingsCurrency` is exactly ZMW. USD fee → `different-currency`, true; ZMW fee → `same-currency`, false. With no prior, framing is `present-state`; only the accepted prior USD→current ZMW input uses `changed-from-prior`. No inference from fixture names, amounts, numeric exchange rates, dates, display currency or preference. No FX computation or numerical figure in the read.

**E1–E6 — throw `RangeError`, return no read, no assumption/coercion/fallback:**
- E1: input missing, null or not an object suitable for this record (including arrays).
- E2: savingsCurrency missing or not exactly `'ZMW'` (including USD/empty/lowercase).
- E3: feeFixedCurrency missing or not exactly `'USD'` or `'ZMW'` (including EUR/lowercase).
- E4: priorFeeFixedCurrency present but not exactly USD or ZMW; prior=current; or prior ZMW→current USD. Only prior USD→current ZMW is accepted. C-USD is dropped; rejected input yields no explanation.
- E5: any unknown keys, including amount, savingsAmount, feeAmount, displayCurrency, preference, rate or contribution; no silent dropping of amount-bearing or preference data.
- E6: asOf present but not a valid ISO timestamp with a zone. Match sarah-read-v1’s validObservation rule: string, real YYYY-MM-DD calendar date, T, valid hour/minute and optional seconds/fraction, Z or explicit ±HH:MM zone, finite parse. Reject impossible dates and missing zone; never normalize or fill from a clock.

**Three Founder-locked literals — character-exact, U+2019 apostrophes; no interpolation:**
- **P-USD:** Sarah’s savings are in Zambian kwacha, while her course fee is fixed in US dollars. The fee’s kwacha cost depends on the example exchange rate.
- **P-ZMW:** Sarah’s savings are in Zambian kwacha, and her course fee is fixed in the same currency. The fee’s kwacha cost does not depend on the example exchange rate.
- **C-ZMW:** Sarah’s savings are in Zambian kwacha, and her course fee is now fixed in the same currency. The fee’s kwacha cost no longer depends on the example exchange rate.

**Selection:** P-USD only for USD fee with no prior; P-ZMW only for ZMW fee with no prior; C-ZMW only for prior USD→current ZMW (savings ZMW). No other explanation or direction is authorised. Sue’s P-ZMW wording aligns with sarah-read-v1’s `The exchange rate does not change this declared amount.`; neither changes that existing string nor compares fee figures.

**Locked explanation-only pinning rule (every accepted variant):**
1. Raw equality to the three independent literal goldens; no trimming, case folding, apostrophe replacement or Unicode normalization. Exact equality also rejects spelled-out figures and paraphrases. Never generate expected strings from the function or share runtime constants with goldens.
2. Reject Unicode Number `/\p{N}/u`, Unicode Currency Symbol `/\p{Sc}/u`, U+2014 `/\u2014/u`, and standalone/K-prefixed currency figure tokens `/\bK(?:\b|(?=\p{N}))/iu`. `kwacha` remains valid.
3. Reject case-insensitive occurrences, including within longer words: `/(?:your|safe|stable|affordable|afford|mistake|success|warning|risk|should|recommend|enough|cover|progress|hedge|hedging)/iu`. Negation does not exempt a match.
4. Reject standalone ISO codes `/\b(?:USD|ZMW)\b/iu` in explanation prose. Names Zambian kwacha/US dollars remain locked.
5. P-USD/P-ZMW reject `/\bnow\b/iu` and `/\bno\s+longer\b/iu`; C-ZMW contains exactly one of each in the pinned positions and only the supported transition/framing.
6. Future rendering separately pins visible adjacent `This read does not establish whether Sarah can pay the fee.` exactly. Do not apply the explanation lexicon to that required limit or the whole serialised result: structured version/asOf/currency/framing legitimately contain other contract text. Character/lexicon tests supplement exact literals, never authorise future wording; a change requires fresh governed review/Founder lock.

**Named failing-first tests — acceptance criteria in `engine-sarah-currency-match.test.ts` (contract §7 adjusted to the Founder resolution):**
1. `amount independence: denomination-only derivation ignores savings and fee amounts` — external test data varies savings K600/K6,000/K60,000 and fee amounts, derives only the exact denomination input from unchanged U*/Z* examples, and deep-compares whole result/explanation for identical currency/prior/asOf. Amounts are never passed into this API; attempts are E5. Also prove at type level exact input/output keys and absence of any amount/rate/display/preference fields, with no index-signature escape. Identical input → identical read; do not compare outputs with different explicit asOf as if time were absent.
2. `denomination not display: unknown keys are rejected (E5)` — displayCurrency/preference/amount/rate/contribution rejected; fee denomination alone selects relationship/rate dependence/copy. Complete output key set is pinned, asOf omitted or exact echo, no input mutation.
3. `missing or unsupported inputs throw: E1–E6` — named cases for all six errors, including prior=current, rejected ZMW→USD, unsupported prior, present invalid optionals, malformed dates/zones and unknown keys. No output from rejection.
4. `cross-read alignment: original U3 and changed Z3` — use unchanged SARAH_EXAMPLE_INPUTS and computeSarahRead; declaredFee.currency equals derived feeFixedCurrency. U3 example-rate ↔ true/different-currency/P-USD; Z3 declared-zmw ↔ false/same-currency with no prior P-ZMW and prior USD C-ZMW. No duplicate runtime research/story/amount state or sarah-read edit.
5. `no-claim lexicon and exact explanation pinning` — every accepted denomination/prior/asOf variant meets raw literal equality plus all Unicode number/currency-symbol/K-token/standalone USD-ZMW/em-dash/lexicon/hedge-hedging bans above; no numerical figure in words can evade equality. Scan explanation only.
6. `now/no longer only in changed-from-prior framing` — exactly one of each only in C-ZMW, none in P-USD/P-ZMW; E4 rejected directions produce no explanation.
7. `version: sarah-currency-match-v1 and unchanged sarah-read-v1` — returned version equals new module literal constant; existing Sarah version/reads/goldens stay unchanged. New module has no version chooser.
8. `golden: three character-exact explanation literals` — independent literal P-USD/P-ZMW/C-ZMW expected file; compare real returned strings, no normalization/generated snapshot/fourth string.
9. `purity: fake and advanced clock and storage cannot change the read` — same explicit input across fake/advanced clocks with storage access trapped; no storage/clock/provider/network access or input mutation. No-asOf input stays no-asOf. Changing asOf alone changes only echoed asOf; changing fee denomination affects only feeFixedCurrency/relationship/rate dependence/explanation; adding supported prior to ZMW affects only framing/explanation. New Engine read moves nothing in existing reads/consumers.

§346 red → green → refactor: FIRST runtime commit contains only the two new test/golden files and records intended failing reproductions against the absent module; later smallest runtime-only green. No waiver. Preserve red/green history for independent reproduction (normal final squash permitted). All three §346/§347 stability properties are asserted against actual results. A TypeScript-only assertion must be checked by TypeScript, not merely transpiled by Vitest.

**Validation / direct runtime typecheck:** Run focused new Vitest contracts plus unchanged Sarah fee tests/goldens, full local `pnpm run validate`, and direct `pnpm exec tsc --noEmit --strict --skipLibCheck --target ES2020 --module ESNext --moduleResolution Bundler apps/frontend/lib/engine/sarah-currency-match.ts`. Frontend tsconfig reaches lib through consumers and excludes tests; this new module has no consumer, so normal frontend typecheck alone does not reach it. Run an explicit type-level contract check of the allowlisted test assertions (using an ephemeral check under a disposable worktree if needed; no new tracked config/path). Direct typecheck must include the new module. Never repair an excluded reachable dependency or inherited diagnostic silently; stop/report if scope outside the three paths is needed. No user-visible behavior is introduced, so no new route/E2E case or Production browser probe is needed beyond source/import/diff confirmation of no consumer and no visible change. Required hosted E2E smoke still applies.

**Research sequence — later, not activated:** Only the research route is a possible future consumer; no Home consumer. Changed facts → participant interpretation → currency-match reveal → assessment of what the reveal added; retain `Nothing else has changed` on the controlled-change screen. Contributions/totals/countdowns/coverage/savings-vs-fee visual/progress/allocation/action stay outside this experiment. Recognition is comprehension, not evidence of value; stronger later evidence identifies what rate changes no longer affect, keeps payment ability unresolved and names consequential clarification. All this needs its own later brief; research route/stimuli remain unchanged and unreleased.

**Exclusions / §372 change control:** sarah-read.ts, its tests/goldens, existing version/amounts/strings/outputs/math/rates and all Home/research/consumer files stay byte-identical. The new separately authorised module/version does not bump or modify sarah-read-v1; §372 later deliberate Engine change control is retained, not waived by analogy. No EngineState/posture, ledger/wallet/balance, storage/reset/visit, preferences, display normalization, live/backend FX, Daniel, shared helpers, generalized registry, dependency, pack, doctrine/ADR or financial-capability change. No Home rendering/fee selector, research wiring/sequence implementation, S18 sweep, walkthrough, SME, personal input, telemetry, participant release, Lane E/G, D-number/role/delegation, protection/required-check change or provider/configuration action. Outside the version-limited carve-out, full §343 applies; §347 and accepted ADRs 0013/0014/0027/AGENTS remain controlling. Research unreleased. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

**Source gates / stops / runtime / closeout:** Source docs/authority-fixture commit → separate deterministic branch RAP commit → local validate/authority/allowlist/literal checks → draft source PR → owner-account update if behind main and current-head checks start → ready/§331 squash auto-merge armed → fresh distinct independent GPT-6.1 Sol high exact-head PASS and hosted required validate/E2E smoke/hedgr-verifier success → source merge → separate sole-JSON permanent-main RAP PR (§364) with its own fresh distinct independent GPT-6.1 Sol high exact-head PASS and required hosted gates/governed merge → fetch main/bridge:rap:check PASS → activation effective/Steward evidence and stop. No direct/admin merge or Steward attestation; any new head invalidates PASS and needs fresh independent verification. If all green/CLEAN stalls ~5 minutes, the steward who armed auto-merge may disable/re-enable squash auto-merge on the unchanged head only. Stop without merging on outside runtime allowlist need (especially sarah-read.ts/Home/research), any unlocked string, authority conflict, required missing context or two Verifier FAILs.

Dex then launches separate GPT-6.1 Sol xhigh Implementer → tests-only red first → smallest Engine-only green → focused contracts/unchanged Sarah checks/direct typechecks/full validate → fresh independent GPT-6.1 Sol high exact-head review (reproduce red, all named tests and preservation), required hosted gates/verifier readback and §331 governed merge → fetched-main post-runtime RAP check (rebind only if needed) → ordinary exact-revision frontend Production READY evidence and no-user-visible-change confirmation (no consumer, no Production browser probe) → separately verified source technical closeout → final separate independently verified permanent-main RAP rebind/fetched-main RAP PASS. Close only this nested ticket, both parents stay open, no successor activation. Single runtime revert for rollback; authority reversal uses governed source and RAP. No deployment/promotion or research release is authorised by this Steward activation task. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

#### Exact runtime/test edit allowlist (21 paths; §380)

Runtime (six existing paths; no other runtime edit):
- `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx` — existing journey own-sim card/since/limits, state-aware hero inclusion, shared split/composed caption use, existing event-date presentation props, optional amount-free decorative sibling using existing classes; posture-region integration only, no state-selection change.

### Completed nested Lane V — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-STABILITY-SARAH-READ-001` (closed §373 on source merge and final rebind)

**Completion / effect:** §373 records #856 delivery of the bounded fee read and `sarah-read-v1`, exact-head independent PASS, unchanged post-runtime RAP and exact-revision READY Production. No Production browser probe is required by this brief because there is no user-visible consumer. This §372 brief remains completed history after source closeout merge and final separate independently verified permanent-main RAP rebind (§364); it grants no continuing runtime permission. Close only this nested ticket; both parents remain open with no active nested successor or activation, and research remains unreleased.

**Authority / start:** Founder decisions and verbatim locks are recorded in §372. This assignment grants Repo Steward (§9.3) ACT_WITH_CONFIRMATION; Dex coordinates as Engineering Operator (§9.11). Sole nested Lane V under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; `SE-REASON-001` stays open without a nested ticket and G stays deferred. Source activation has no runtime code. Effective activation requires permanent-main source merge, separate independently verified projection-only RAP rebind and successful `bridge:rap:check` on fetched main (§364). No TDD waiver: implementation is launched separately by Dex using GPT-6.1 Sol xhigh; independent verification uses GPT-6.1 Sol high.

**Objective / exact input contract:** Export one pure deterministic `computeSarahRead(input: SarahReadInput): SarahRead` from the new Engine module. Input has exactly `declaredFee: { amount: number, currency: 'USD' | 'ZMW', dueDate: string }`, `fixtureRateZmwPerUsd: number`, and `asOf: string`. Amount is finite and non-negative; rate is explicit, finite and positive, including for ZMW observations, but ZMW computation does not use it. Deadline is an explicit valid calendar date in `YYYY-MM-DD`; the six approved examples use `2027-10-01`. `asOf` is an explicit valid ISO timestamp with a timezone, not a Date object, number, missing value or ambient clock. Reject impossible calendar dates, missing/invalid fields, unsupported currency, invalid rate, negative/non-finite amount and rounded ngwee overflow with `RangeError` and no read. No guessing, date normalization to another calendar day, coercion, fallback or partially returned read. The deadline supplies context only, never a forecast or countdown.

**Exact output contract / one computation path:** Top-level keys are exactly `engineVersion`, `asOf`, `declaredFee`, `localAmountZmwMinor`, `localDisplay`, `rateBasis`, `explanation`. `engineVersion` is the module constant `SARAH_READ_ENGINE_VERSION = 'sarah-read-v1'`; caller cannot choose it. `asOf` preserves the explicit input string. `declaredFee` preserves amount/currency/deadline and adds only `source: 'user-declared'` and `access: 'read-only'`; this is a fictional fee declaration, not custody, wealth, a holding, balance, provider quote or evidence of payment ability. `localAmountZmwMinor` is one JS safe-integer number of ngwee, non-negative. `localDisplay` comes from those same BigInt minor units using fixed `en-US`, `K` with no space, grouping and meaningful two-digit fractional minor units (omit `.00`). No floating-point kwacha field or second numerical representation.

USD: multiply declared USD fee by the explicit fixture ZMW/USD rate using decimal-to-BigInt fractions and non-negative half-a-local-cent-up rounding, matching Daniel's pattern; guard against `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` before conversion. `rateBasis` is exactly `{ kind: 'example-rate', zmwPerUsd: input.fixtureRateZmwPerUsd }`. `explanation` has exactly `fee`, `rateAssumption`, `limits`.

ZMW: convert the declared kwacha fee directly to rounded BigInt ngwee, with the same rounding/overflow/display rule, without FX multiplication or division. `rateBasis` is exactly `{ kind: 'declared-zmw' }`; the irrelevant rate is absent from the read. `explanation` has exactly `fee`, `limits`; omit the `rateAssumption` key entirely, not null/empty wording. No division of K29,500 by USD 1,000 to recover 29.5. No comparison, sequence, trend, progress, sufficiency or action output; U1–U3/Z1–Z3 are six independent observations, not one timeline read.

**Permitted input-change paths:** Identical inputs deep-equal, with no input mutation. Vary rate alone at fixed amount/currency/deadline/asOf: USD may change only `localAmountZmwMinor`, `localDisplay`, `rateBasis.zmwPerUsd`, `explanation.fee` and `explanation.rateAssumption`; ZMW read stays deep-equal, including its direct rate basis and omitted rate line. Vary amount: declared amount plus amount-derived minor units/display/fee explanation may change, nothing else. Vary denomination alone: declared currency, fee-derived minor units/display/fee explanation, rate basis and presence/value of the USD-only rate line may change, nothing else (preserve amount, deadline, asOf, version, source/access, limits). Vary deadline alone: declared deadline and the formatted deadline in `explanation.fee` only. Vary `asOf` alone: `asOf` only. A later deliberate Engine change needs separately authorised version/change control; these three locked stability properties (§346/§347) are tests, not claims about Sarah's capital.

**Current source / examples:** The module may expose `SARAH_CURRENT_FIXTURE_RATE_ZMW_PER_USD` only as an alias derived by importing the existing `DANIEL_FIXTURE_RATE_ZMW_PER_USD`; no second current literal, live fetch or Daniel file edit. Current U3/Z3 example inputs use that alias (literal expected 27 in test goldens). Historical 24.5 and 29.5 and the frozen dates below are explicit example-history inputs, following §365 decision 2, never alternate current rates. The module may hold the six labelled fictional example input declarations for tests/future separately authorised consumption; no Home consumer or registry is introduced. Runtime never imports test goldens or research figures; Engine-owned goldens below are literal expected test data, not outputs generated from `computeSarahRead`.

**Locked §7a copy register — five Founder-approved items, verbatim before runtime:**
1. USD fee: `Course fee USD {amount}, due {dueDate}, shows as {localDisplay} at this example rate.`
2. ZMW fee: `Course fee K{amount}, due {dueDate}, is set in kwacha. The exchange rate does not change this declared amount.`
3. Rate line (USD fees only; omitted for ZMW fees): `Example rate: ZMW {r} per US dollar. Not a live rate.`
4. Shared D2 sentence (both currencies): `Fictional course fee. This figure is not a payment quote. This read does not establish whether Sarah can pay the fee.`
5. Formats: `{D} {Mon} {YYYY}` (e.g. 1 Oct 2027); `USD {amount}`; `K{amount}`; fixed en-US grouping.

**Named red→green Acceptance Criteria (§346, no waiver; Sarah Vitest contract test):**
1. `replays identical fee inputs to a deep-equal Sarah read` — six full literal goldens, repeat calls, exact keys/values and input immutability.
2. `USD fee responds only to the rate` — at fixed asOf/deadline/amount vary 24.5 / 29.5 / current Engine constant; only the permitted leaf paths above change; 2,450,000 / 2,950,000 / 2,700,000 ngwee. Separately verify all three literal observation dates.
3. `ZMW fee ignores the rate` — fixed asOf/deadline/fee yields a deep-equal read at all three rates, 2,950,000 ngwee / K29,500, direct basis and no rate line.
4. `fee denomination changes only fee-derived outputs` — vary currency alone at the same numeric amount and other inputs; assert exact allowed path changes and preserved limits/deadline/asOf/version/source/access. The U/Z story pair also changes amount, so is not the one-input control.
5. `never infers a rate from the ZMW fee` — vary the ZMW fee and explicit unrelated rates; direct cents only, no ratio or inferred 29.5 basis/output.
6. `uses the locked explanation strings` — all USD/ZMW worked strings, USD-only rate line entirely omitted for ZMW, shared D2 sentence, exact date/grouping/fraction formatting.
7. `rejects invalid dates, amounts and overflow without a read` — missing/non-string/impossible dates, unsupported currency, non-finite/negative amounts, missing/non-positive/non-finite rates, both denomination overflow boundaries; valid zero/control and safe-integer boundary. Throw RangeError, no partial read.
8. `rounds exact minor units` — literal USD 1.005 at rate 1 → 101 ngwee / K1.01; USD 0.05 at 0.1 → 1 / K0.01; USD 0.04 at 0.1 → 0 / K0; ZMW 1.005 → 101 / K1.01; K1.50 retains two minor-unit digits. Same BigInt cents supply canonical number and display; no binary floating-point half-cent shortcut.
9. `reports sarah-read-v1` — module version and all computed/golden reads; exact output envelope blocks undeclared fields.
10. `excludes progress, comparison, sufficiency and action claims` — recursive key and string scans across all goldens/computed controls plus source review; retain §347 hedge/hedging guard and §343 prohibited phrases/paraphrases. Ban gaps, percentages, coverage, targets, on-track/closer claims, savings/contribution relationships, custody/verified-wealth, action/permission/CTA/route keys. Assert locked negative sufficiency sentence exactly; do not falsely ban its necessary denial by matching the words “can pay” alone. No historical tracking or comparison output.
11. `current rate derives from the Engine constant` — source import/alias and current U3/Z3 input evidence plus mocked constant control; current alias changes with the Daniel constant, historical 24.5 / 29.5 stay fixed; literal goldens still expect 27. No second current literal or Daniel edit.
12. `ignores ambient clock storage and display preference` — change clock, local/session storage and display preference without changing read; no ambient access, fetch, provider or storage imports. Repeated explicit time, including deadline independence, remains deterministic.
13. `deadline and observation changes stay isolated` — deadline alone changes only deadline/fee prose; asOf alone changes only asOf. The current time supplies no deadline/progress/forecast computation.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop/report without merging on two Verifier FAILs, genuine current-authority conflict, any Home runtime need, wording beyond S7/S8 or discovery/scope beyond Daniel's Engine and its tests. Also stop for missing context, out-of-allowlist need, changed trust/data/release conditions, explicit hold or concrete gate blocker. One FAIL permits bounded correction then fresh exact-head review. Expansion needs Founder source amendment and separate verified permanent-main rebind. Runtime rollback is one revert of its PR, no migration; authority rollback is governed source/rebind. Preserve primary checkout / unrelated artifacts. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

Cross-reference: §370; §346 / §347 / §350 / §351 / §357 / §358 / §364 / §367 / §368 / §369; AGENTS §9.3 / §9.11 and runbook PR Posture.

1. `renders the same Daniel read on all three simulated Home routes` (Vitest and Playwright): same eligibility and separate near-hero panel on all three paths, with empty/settled/pending/failed simulated activity; no journey-only gating. Hero remains its existing source and hierarchy.
2. `shows Engine localDisplay above its caption with the complete runtime fixture disclosure` (Vitest and Playwright): exact `K21,600`; smaller `display estimate` below the figure; fictional user-declared USD 800, USD/ZMW, exact Engine `explanation.rateAssumption`, fixed explicit `asOf`; computed fixture asserts `daniel-read-v2` internally. No deferred holding explanation. Assert no text parsing or second amount computation via a presentation boundary/read fixture test.
3. `keeps Daniel separate from mock guidance and preserves every notice text` (Vitest, all existing posture variants; Playwright visible normal control): panel carries both coexistence qualifications; notice title/body equal canonical existing strings; existing posture/guidance is unaffected. No influence, aggregation or override. No notice-prose parsing.
4. `keeps the Daniel read identical when display currency or simulated activity changes` (Vitest and Playwright): all five currencies and deposit/withdraw/pending/failed/returning states; deep-equal Engine read and character-identical Daniel figure/disclosures. Same fixture inputs give same read, ambient clock/storage changes cannot affect it; changing display preference or activity changes nothing in Daniel (§346/§347 three-property contract).
5. `omits Daniel entirely in live mode and preserves live Home` (Vitest; no live sign-in/network in E2E): absent panel, no new fixture disclosure, retained live markup/copy; explicit journey query cannot bypass live eligibility.
6. `imports the Engine ZMW fixture while preserving all other display and fixed FX rates` (`simulation-display-currency.test.tsx` and `synthetic-journey-state.test.ts`): assert ZMW equals imported Engine constant and literal 27; inspect/import dependency so a coincidentally equal second literal fails. KES/NGN/GHS/PHP display values unchanged; helper non-ZMW supported quotes unchanged; fixed ZMW `getFixedRate('ZMW')` and `FX_RATE_ZMW_PER_USD_DEFAULT` stay literal 20.
7. `normalizes new synthetic ZMW deposit rows at the Engine fixture without changing the live API boundary` (`deposit.page.test.tsx`, Playwright): synthetic input 135 ZMW previews/confirms USD 5 and persists new-row `fx_rate` from imported constant / `amount_zmw` 135; synthetic path sends no deposit API request. Boundary controls retain fixed-FX 20/default route behavior. A seeded old 20 row remains byte-identical.
8. `normalizes non-ZMW synthetic deposit ZMW legs at the Engine fixture with unchanged selected-currency amounts` (`deposit.page.test.tsx`, `display-currency.spec.ts`, new Playwright normalization case): parameterize KES 650, NGN 7500, GHS 75, PHP 280; USD 5 remains USD 5, selected rate/currency unchanged; new ZMW fields equal imported 27/135. Readback Activity/detail respects existing hiding rules and displays the stored value where already eligible; no runtime UI edits. This fails against old normalization 20.
9. `shows synthetic withdrawal ZMW estimates at the Engine fixture while preserving USD and lifecycle semantics` (`withdraw.page.test.tsx`, `display-currency.spec.ts`): USD 5→ZMW 135, USD input/available limit/confirm amount unchanged; all other display rates remain unchanged, zero technical withdrawal local fields remain unchanged. Existing stored records are not rewritten.
10. `contains no prohibited claims in Daniel Home copy` (Vitest plus Playwright panel-copy check): §347 hedge/hedging guard; §343 obligation-progress fence; no custody, verified wealth, sufficiency, guarantee, recommendation, action permission or balance-held claim. Fixture/time/rate limits accompany the read. Preserve existing notice text rather than applying the Daniel guard as a reason to rewrite notices.
11. `Daniel panel reflows without clipping or overflow on all three routes` (Playwright): all three routes at 390 px / 100% and 320 px / 200% text, five currencies, empty and changed-activity states; figure/caption order, disclosure visibility and keyboard accessibility, no horizontal overflow or financial progress/meter/gamification element.
12. FIRST runtime commit is test-only: named failing tests and only the affected rate-dependent existing assertions/fixtures/imports above, with retained red output against unchanged runtime. Later smallest green runtime makes them pass; keep red and green commits visible for independent review (normal final squash allowed). Focused Vitest, local `pnpm run validate` and frontend `e2e:ci`, hosted `validate` and `E2E smoke (@hedgr/frontend)` must pass on exact head. Verifier reproduces named red/green evidence. No test makes live external calls.

**Exclusions:** No Engine function/type/version/golden change; no `EngineState`, mock provider, notices, posture, target allocation, ledger maths/schema/migration/old-row rewrite or ledger-to-Daniel input. No new withdrawal conversion, new route/component stylesheet, live/default non-journey Deposit/Withdraw FX change, `lib/fx.ts`, API/backend/TrustDisclosureBanner/zmwToUsd change, research stimuli/route release, locked pack, holding explanation, K17,500 local portion, Sarah/SME, other drift fix, new dependency, Worker/provider config, protocol/Form, telemetry, personal data, participant release, doctrine/ADR, D-number, Lane E/G or financial capability. Activity/TxDetail runtime files and fixture-only stored-row tests (`activity.page.test.tsx`, `TxDetailModal.test.tsx`, ledger/tx lifecycle) stay unchanged because they consume existing records rather than generate new synthetic rows.

**Sequence / all gates:** Source commit → separate deterministic branch RAP commit → local validate plus source authority/record-order checks → draft source PR → owner-account branch update if behind main and current-head checks start → ready / governed squash auto-merge armed → fresh independent GPT-6.1 Sol high Verifier PASS on exact head → successful `hedgr/verifier` readback and required current-head checks → source merge → separate projection-only permanent-main RAP rebind PR with its own fresh GPT-6.1 Sol high independent Verifier and same gates → fetch main and successful `bridge:rap:check` → activation effective; stewardship stops → Dex launches separate Implementer from effective main → test-only red first runtime commit → minimal green implementation → local/hosted exact-head checks, fresh independent PASS, verifier readback and governed runtime merge → separate verified post-runtime permanent-main RAP rebind if provenance checks require → exact-revision ordinary frontend deployment READY and public-alias probe of all three simulated Home routes at 390 / 320@200% (panel/disclosure/coexistence/hero, synthetic rate/new-row checks; preserve existing production storage) → source-first technical closeout with independent gates → final separate verified permanent-main RAP rebind. Close only this nested ticket; both parents stay open, no automatic successor/research release. Every PR follows runbook PR Posture; one PR in merge step at a time, no bypass/protection change. Author/coordinator never attest. Each Verifier is READ_ONLY except exactly one expressly permitted runbook `Hedgr-Verifier: PASS|FAIL sha=<40hex> run=<verifier-url>` comment after immediately re-reading the live head; abort on mismatch. A changed head invalidates PASS and requires fresh independent verification. Confirm required hosted checks finish and re-read final `hedgr/verifier` success before reporting merge gates satisfied.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop for authority conflict, missing context, scope beyond D1–D5 plus A/A1 and the rate decision, out-of-allowlist need, further unaccepted currency coupling, Activity/TxDetail runtime change, live/backend or existing-row change, claim/data/release fence breach, explicit hold or concrete gate blocker. FAIL pauses merge for bounded correction; two independent Verifier FAILs stop/report without merging. New scope needs explicit Founder source amendment, merge and separate verified rebind before runtime continuation. Runtime rollback is one revert of its PR; authority rollback is a separate governed source/rebind sequence. Preserve unrelated files, primary checkout/output/tmp and worktrees; no clean, hard reset or forced removal. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

Cross-reference: §359; §327, §343, §346–§347, §350–§351, §355–§358, D-132 / §264, functional contract “Scoped semantic interface disposition”, ADRs 0013/0014/0015/0027. Historical narrower consumer/table exclusions are not continuing scope for this new ticket.

### Completed nested Lane V — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-STABILITY-DANIEL-AMOUNT-001` (closed §358 on source merge and final rebind)

**Authority / start:** Founder Musalwa Hibajene's direct selection and D1–D3 answers of 9 Oct 2026 (AWST), recorded faithfully in §357; Dex coordinated as Engineering Operator (§352 / AGENTS §9.11). Sole nested Lane V under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; Stability layer; Class A informational/synthetic; Green classification only, no standing delegation. `SE-REASON-001` remains open without a nested ticket; G deferred. No D-number. Source permanent-main merge and its separate independently verified projection-only RAP rebind must complete, followed by successful `bridge:rap:check` on fetched permanent main, before the separate Implementer starts runtime. This Repo Steward assignment stops at effective activation. Verified PRs may auto-merge under §331 without additional Founder approval per merge.

**Objective / exact outputs:** Extend only the shipped pure `computeDanielRead` / separate `DanielRead` with exactly one new top-level field: `localAmountZmwMinor`, a JS `number` counting integer ngwee (ZMW minor units), e.g. K21,600 → `2160000`. Both this field and `localDisplay` derive from the same existing rounded BigInt `cents`; no independent calculation or parse of display text supplies the field. Compare those cents to `BigInt(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)` before number conversion; above it, throw `RangeError` and return no read. No decimal string or kwacha float (`Number(cents)/100`). Retain decimal BigInt arithmetic, the non-negative half-a-local-cent-up rule and fixed `en-US` display formatting. Preserve all existing valid-input display and explanation strings. Change only `DANIEL_READ_ENGINE_VERSION` from `daniel-read-v1` to `daniel-read-v2`, carried in the read. Invalid/missing/non-string `asOf` remains rejected with `RangeError`.

**Explicit §350 §7a AC2-style amendment (this ticket only):** On a fixture-rate change, the only moving leaf paths are `localAmountZmwMinor`, `localDisplay`, `explanation.rateAssumption`, and `explanation.holding`. On a declared-USD holding change, the only moving leaf paths are `declaredHolding.amount`, `localAmountZmwMinor`, `localDisplay`, and `explanation.holding`. The version changes deliberately between v1 and v2, never because an input varies. No other output key may be added at any depth. Exact top-level keys after this slice: `engineVersion`, `asOf`, `pair`, `declaredHolding`, `localAmountZmwMinor`, `localDisplay`, `explanation`; existing nested keys and semantics are unchanged. This supersedes the narrower historical AC2 output list solely for this ticket and addresses §351 B1. Explanation templates/text are unchanged; their interpolated amounts and disclosed rate still follow declared inputs. The guarded integer-minor-unit representation addresses §351 B2; B3's shipped `typeof asOf` guard is retained.

**Exact file allowlist:**
- Source activation and later closeout: `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` (live §7/§7a and numbered records), `AGENTS.md` (one short live pointer only), `apps/bridge-worker/tests/fork1-authority.test.mjs` (exact sequencing fixture only; necessary because it asserts the entire projected live sequencing sentence), and separately committed generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` after the source commit.
- Runtime: `apps/frontend/lib/engine/daniel-read.ts` (existing single computation path, input/read types and version constant only).
- Runtime tests: `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-daniel-read.test.ts` and `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-daniel-read.golden.ts` (existing Vitest contract and engine-owned literal golden grid only). No fourth runtime/test file.
- Each separate permanent-main RAP rebind: `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` only.
- Evidence outside tracked source: `/Users/musalwa/Projects/hedgr-copilot/output/daniel-amount-20261009/` only.

**Named red→green Acceptance Criteria (§346; no waiver):** All named tests are in `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-daniel-read.test.ts`.
1. `exposes exact safe-integer ngwee across the engine-owned golden grid`: every literal golden read contains `localAmountZmwMinor` as a number and safe integer; baseline 800 × 27 → K21,600 / 2160000; 29.5 → K23,600 / 2360000; 24.5 → K19,600 / 1960000; holding control 1000 × 27 → K27,000 / 2700000. Add at least the fractional-cent case 1.005 × 1 → K1.01 / 101 to the literal grid. Preserve existing expected strings; expected values are literal, never generated by the function under test.
2. `keeps localAmountZmwMinor and localDisplay identical in minor units`: assert single-source identity across the grid and rounding controls (1.005 × 1 → 101, 0.05 × 0.1 → 1, 0.04 × 0.1 → 0, 1000.5 × 27 → 2701350). Independently decode the display in the test to integer minor units and compare with the returned field; the runtime field must originate from the same BigInt cents, not decoded text or recomputed float. Fixed-locale and trailing-cent formatting remain unchanged.
3. `throws RangeError without a read above the safe-integer cents limit`: near-limit safe cents still return an exact safe integer; cents above `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` throw `RangeError` with no returned read. Include a finite input just above the limit (90071992547409.92 × 1 → 9007199254740992 cents) and a finite large overflow (e.g. `Number.MAX_VALUE` × 27); a valid near-limit control 90071992547409.9 × 1 → 9007199254740990 cents must remain exact. The guard compares BigInt cents before conversion.
4. `reports daniel-read-v2 as the explicit engine version`: assert the constant and every read/golden version equal literal `daniel-read-v2` (not merely equality to a mutable constant).
5. `adds only localAmountZmwMinor to the Daniel read output envelope`: assert the exact top-level key set above and exact existing nested key sets; every legacy golden field/string is unchanged except the deliberate version bump. No other key at any depth.
6. P2 FX — `changes only the canonical local amount display and rate explanation when the fixture rate changes`: update the existing FX test's name and leaf-path assertion to the four authorised paths above; all other fields remain equal. P2 holding — retain `changes only holding-derived fields when the declared USD holding changes`, adding only `localAmountZmwMinor` to its leaf-path assertion. These tests fail against v1 and pass only with the authorised field.
7. Regression / three-property contract — retain green `replays identical declared inputs to a deep-equal Daniel read`, `ignores ambient clock storage and display preference`, `matches the engine-owned golden fixture grid` (deliberate field/version/fractional-case updates only), `reuses BigInt cents half-cent-up rounding with fixed en-US formatting`, `returns no read for an invalid or missing asOf`, `contains no prohibited language in slice strings`, and `exposes no action permission or balance-held claims`. Preserve input immutability, replay, isolation and the existing language/envelope assertions. An engine change moves nothing else unless deliberate (§346 / §347).
8. FIRST runtime commit contains only failing tests and the literal golden updates in the two allowed test files; retain command/output proving red against unchanged v1 runtime, before a later minimal green implementation commit. Test typing may accommodate the absent v1 field within the test files only; no runtime edit in the red commit. Keep red and green commits visible for independent review (normal final squash allowed). Focused Vitest, local `pnpm run validate` and frontend `e2e:ci` pass; hosted `validate` and `E2E smoke (@hedgr/frontend)` pass on the exact head. No new Playwright surface test: this slice adds no user-visible surface.

**Exclusions (D3):** Engine-only. No Home, route, UI or consumer change; Daniel on Home stays a separate future decision. No `EngineState`, posture, notices, ledger/balance/allocation behaviour, live FX, app FX table or `scenario-fixtures.ts` edit. Preserve one user-declared USD holding, USD/ZMW and disclosed fixture baseline 27; no K17,500 local portion, multiple holdings/pairs, new dependency, new abstraction or second computation path. Storage, clock, display preference/metadata and explanation/copy are not computation inputs. No locked `docs/strategy/stability-engine-pack.md` edit, doctrine/ADR change, D-number, Sarah/SME, other drift item, research/participant release, collection, telemetry, Lane E/G, provider/Worker configuration, financial capability or cross-lane authority. Declared/read-only semantics remain informational, never custody, accounting truth, settled amount or verified wealth (ADRs 0013/0014/0015/0027).

**Sequence / all gates:** Source commit → separate deterministic branch RAP commit → local validate and precedent authority checks → draft source PR → owner-account branch update if behind main and checks start on the current head → ready / governed auto-merge armed → fresh independent GPT-6.1 Sol high Verifier PASS on exact head → successful exact-head `hedgr/verifier` readback and all required checks → source merge → separate projection-only permanent-main RAP rebind PR with its own fresh independent exact-head Verifier PASS and governed merge → fetch permanent main and successful `bridge:rap:check` → activation effective and stewardship stops → Dex launches a separate Implementer from effective current main → test-only red first runtime commit → minimal green runtime → runtime exact-head checks, independent PASS, verifier readback and governed merge → separate verified post-runtime permanent-main RAP rebind if provenance checks require → confirm exact-revision ordinary frontend deployment READY (no Production probe: no user-visible surface) → source-first technical closeout with independent gates → final separate verified permanent-main RAP rebind. Close only this nested ticket; both parents remain open, no automatic successor. Every PR follows runbook PR Posture, one PR in merge step at a time; no bypass or protection change. Author/coordinator never attest. Each independent Verifier is READ_ONLY except exactly one expressly permitted runbook `Hedgr-Verifier: PASS|FAIL sha=<40hex> run=<url>` comment after immediately re-reading the live head and aborting on mismatch. A changed head invalidates PASS and requires fresh independent verification. §331 allows gated auto-merge without another Founder approval per merge.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop for genuine current-authority disagreement, missing required context, an out-of-allowlist need, scope beyond D1–D3, a record/ID collision that cannot be resolved, changed trust/data/release conditions, explicit hold or a concrete gate blocker. **Objective / in scope (one Home experience, §327: default `/dashboard` and the journey `/dashboard?journey=class-a-val-002` / `/dashboard-synthetic-journey`; no journey-only prop, class or branch is added):**
1. Position line: do not render the dashed max guide (`positionLineGuide`) or the max label (`positionLineTop`) on any route. No replacement guide, target or label.

The sign or wording carries direction. No rust/terracotta and no coloured arrows. No copy change. Other emphasis-coloured elements (post-visit line, visit marker/label, since-link arrow, observation label) are unchanged.
3. The change chip (`dashboard-change-chip`) does not render while the What changed or Since you were last here card is visible. At `2888cb5` every state that renders the chip on either route also renders the Since card (the chip needs `sinceSummary`, which needs a derivable simulated position, and both simulated routes then render that card; live mode never has `sinceSummary`). The rule is therefore implemented as removing the chip from Home on both routes. This supersedes the HOME-EXPERIENCE T3 chip and T5 chip fade on both routes. The hero count-up, line settle and arrival announcement remain.
4. Start-here step 3 offers "Simulate a withdrawal" on both routes. Journey utilities: slot 1 "Add simulated deposit" is unchanged; slot 2 is the one contextual next action, by completed simulated entries: 0 → "How this simulation works"; 1 → "Simulate a withdrawal" (`dashboard-simulated-withdraw`, existing approved copy) linking to the journey `/withdraw`; ≥2 → "View Activity". Default-route utilities (deposit, View Activity, Simulate a withdrawal) already offer the withdrawal at every step and stay as they are (§327 retained default element). Plain links only: no ticks, completion counts, step numbers, percentages or progress.
5. Hero local estimate: wherever the hero renders a local display estimate (today the journey; default Home renders none), inside the existing `local-balance` element the figure (e.g. "≈ NGN 720,000.00") sits on its own line and "display estimate" is a small caption directly under it. `local-balance` textContent and `formatSimulationDisplayEstimate` output stay character-identical. The withdraw page is unchanged.
6. Position-line axis: render no date label and no "Today" on any route. Even event spacing (`(index+1)/(events+1)`) is unchanged.
Live-mode Home (no simulation) renders exactly as at `2888cb5`. Start-here step copy and step circles/markers are unchanged.

**Exact file allowlist:**
- Source activation and later closeout: `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` (live §7/§7a and numbered records), `AGENTS.md` (live echo only), `apps/bridge-worker/tests/fork1-authority.test.mjs` (exact sequencing fixture only), and the separately committed generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` after the source commit.
- Runtime: `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/PositionLine.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/synthetic-home.module.css`, `apps/frontend/components/BalanceWithLocalEstimate.tsx` (one opt-in prop; default rendering unchanged), `apps/frontend/lib/state/simulation-display-currency.ts` (figure/caption parts only; composed output unchanged).
- Runtime tests: `apps/frontend/__tests__/dashboard.page.test.tsx`, `apps/frontend/__tests__/home-small-fixes.test.tsx` (new), `apps/frontend/__tests__/balance-with-local-estimate.test.tsx`, `apps/frontend/__tests__/simulation-display-currency.test.tsx`, `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/home-small-fixes.spec.ts` (new), `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/class-a-val-002.spec.ts` (only the chip, T5 chip-sync, axis and journey first-event utility assertions superseded here).
- Each separate permanent-main RAP rebind: `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` only.
- Evidence outside tracked source: `/Users/musalwa/Projects/hedgr-copilot/output/home-small-fixes-20261009/`.

**Named red-to-green Acceptance Criteria (§346; no waiver):** Vitest names live in `apps/frontend/__tests__/home-small-fixes.test.tsx` unless stated. Playwright names live in `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/home-small-fixes.spec.ts`. Each fails on `2888cb5` and passes on the head. Every route-sensitive test runs on both the default route and the journey.
1. `Home position line draws no max guide or max label on either route` (Vitest) and `Home line shows no dashed guide or max label on either route` (Playwright).
2. `Home change amounts use the sentence ink, not emphasis, on either route` (Playwright; What changed and Since cards; computed colour equals the parent sentence colour and is not rgb(150, 63, 34); no ↑/↓ glyph on Home).
3. `Home hides the change chip while the since card shows on either route` (Vitest; change and no-change) and `Home shows no change chip after a deposit or withdrawal on either route` (Playwright).
4. `journey utilities offer the withdrawal at step 3 and one contextual action per step` (Vitest; 0/1/≥2 entries; exactly one of how-works/withdraw/view-activity; no tick, count, percentage or progressbar) and `Home after the first deposit offers Simulate a withdrawal on either route` (Playwright).

Guards (green on base and head): `attribution stays in the change card after the explanation` (both routes), `default Home keeps its retained elements` (three default utilities, context line, Recent activity rules, Start-here steps and markers, no local estimate), `live Home is unchanged`, `Home adds no totals meters progress gap or gain-loss colour` (both routes).

Later minimal implementation makes them green; keep the red and green commits visible (normal final squash merge allowed). **Sequence / all gates:** Source commit → separate deterministic branch RAP commit → local validate, draft PR, owner-account update if behind main, current-head checks → distinct independent Verifier PASS on exact head → successful `hedgr/verifier` readback and required checks → ready → source merge → separate projection-only permanent-main RAP rebind PR with its own independent exact-head PASS and governed merge → fetch permanent main and successful `bridge:rap:check` → separate runtime branch from effective main, test-only red first commit then minimal green implementation → runtime exact-head checks, independent PASS (confirming each named test fails on base and passes on head), verifier readback and governed merge → separate verified post-runtime permanent-main RAP rebind only if `bridge:rap:check` fails → confirm exact-revision ordinary frontend deployment READY and run a public-alias probe of default Home and journey Home at 390 and 320@200 % → source-first technical closeout with independent exact-head gates → final separate verified permanent-main RAP rebind; focus then returns to Stability Engine work. Every PR uses the runbook; one PR in merge step at a time; no protection bypass. Author/coordinator never attest.
