**Authority / start:** Direct Founder approval to activate and implement, 6 Oct 2026 (AWST), recorded in §350. The full bounded source/RAP/runtime/closeout sequence and ordinary automatic frontend deployments are authorised without routine repeat permission. Effective only after source permanent-main merge and its separate independently verified projection-only RAP rebind; confirm `bridge:rap:check` on permanent main before runtime. Sole nested Lane V under open `CLASS-A-VAL-002`; Stability layer; Class A informational/synthetic; Green classification only, no standing delegation. `SE-REASON-001` remains open without a nested ticket; G deferred. No D-number. Inventory §4 is grounding evidence, not authority.

**Objective / in scope:** One pure deterministic Engine function is the one authoritative computation path from declared inputs to a separate `DanielRead`. Keep that type inside the new module; do not extend `EngineState`. Canonical computed Engine fields define the read; Home is presentation with its own tests, but no Home/route surface is in this ticket. Exactly one user-declared USD holding; no K17,500 local portion. Use a named, disclosed fixture ZMW 27/USD baseline (RETAIN 27), with the 29.5/24.5 comparison fixtures: USD 800 → K21,600 / K23,600 / K19,600. Existing app-table ZMW 20 is unchanged. Reuse the decimal-to-BigInt-cents arithmetic and non-negative half-a-local-cent-up rule of `apps/frontend/lib/narrative/simulation-currency-insight.ts` within the single module; fixed `en-US` formatting. No caller recomputes any part. The read depends only on declared holding, fixture rate, explicit `asOf` and Engine version, not storage, ambient clock, display preference, explanation/copy layer or display metadata. Existing `EnginePosture` may be read-only context or omitted; no new posture meaning.

**Exact file allowlist:**
- Source activation and later closeout: `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` (live §7/§7a and numbered records), `AGENTS.md` (live echo only), `apps/bridge-worker/tests/fork1-authority.test.mjs` (exact sequencing fixture only), and separately committed generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` after the source commit.
- Runtime: `apps/frontend/lib/engine/daniel-read.ts` (new; the single computation function and separate input/read types only).
- Runtime tests: `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-daniel-read.test.ts` (new Vitest contract) and `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-daniel-read.golden.ts` (new engine-owned literal expected fixture grid; no generated expected values or second computation path).
- Each separate permanent-main RAP rebind: `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` only.
- Evidence outside tracked source: `/Users/musalwa/Projects/hedgr-copilot/output/daniel-slice-20261006/`.

**Named red-to-green Acceptance Criteria (§346; no waiver):** All named tests are in `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-daniel-read.test.ts`.
1. P1 — `replays identical declared inputs to a deep-equal Daniel read`: same declared holding, fixture rate, explicit `asOf` and Engine version twice gives deep-equal output, without mutating inputs.
2. P2 FX — `changes only the local display and rate explanation when the fixture rate changes`: USD 800 at 27→29.5/24.5 yields K21,600→K23,600/K19,600; only the local display field and what-changed explanation move. Declared USD amount, declared/read-only semantics, pair, explicit `asOf`, Engine version and any read-only posture context remain equal. Rate assumptions are disclosed within the explanation, not an independent changing judgment.
3. P2 holding — `changes only holding-derived fields when the declared USD holding changes`: at the same fixture rate, change only the declared holding; the declared USD amount, derived local display and holding explanation change only as explained; pair, rate assumptions, explicit `asOf`, version and any read-only posture context remain equal.
4. P2 non-inputs — `ignores ambient clock storage and display preference`: change ambient time, stored review keys and display preference while keeping explicit inputs fixed; the entire read stays deep-equal. `asOf` is passed explicitly, never obtained from the clock. Explanation/copy and display metadata cannot supply computation inputs.
5. P3 — `matches the engine-owned golden fixture grid`: literal expected reads at baseline and both FX variations (plus holding-change control) match; a deliberate Engine change must explain any golden update in the same runtime PR. No snapshot generated from the function being tested.
6. Rounding — `reuses BigInt cents half-cent-up rounding with fixed en-US formatting`: cover the decimal half-cent boundary and fixed-locale output independently of display preference.
7. Language — `contains no prohibited language in slice strings`: recursively check read/golden strings against `/\bhedg(e|es|ed|ing)\b/i`.
8. Envelope — `exposes no action permission or balance-held claims`: no action/permission fields, custody, balance-held, settled-amount or verified-wealth wording; amounts remain user-declared, USD-denominated and read-only under ADR 0027 decision 3 and ADR 0013.
9. FIRST RUNTIME COMMIT contains only the named failing tests and literal golden fixtures, with retained red command/output against unchanged runtime. Do not add the runtime module until that commit exists. Later minimal implementation makes them green; keep red and green commits visible for independent review (normal final squash merge allowed). Focused Vitest plus local `pnpm run validate` and frontend `e2e:ci` pass; hosted `validate` and `E2E smoke (@hedgr/frontend)` pass on the exact head. No new Playwright surface test is required because no user-visible surface is added.

**Exclusions:** No Home/route surface, research-route or `scenario-fixtures.ts` edit, live FX, local-portion input, multiple holdings/currency pairs, dependencies or abstractions beyond the one function. No `EngineState` fields, posture, notices, allocation/ledger/balance behaviour or existing app FX table change. No locked pack edit, doctrine/ADR change, Sarah/SME, inventory drift items 2–5, research/participant release, personal input collection, telemetry, Lane E/G, provider/Worker configuration, financial capability or cross-lane authority. Existing `EngineState` may be read-only context only. No second meaning under `EnginePosture`.

**Sequence / all gates:** Source commit → separate deterministic branch RAP commit → local validate, draft PR, owner-account update if behind main, current-head checks → ready / governed auto-merge armed → distinct independent GPT-6.1 Sol high Verifier PASS on exact head → successful `hedgr/verifier` readback and required checks → source merge → separate projection-only permanent-main RAP rebind PR with its own independent exact-head PASS and governed merge → fetch permanent main and successful `bridge:rap:check` → separate runtime branch from effective main, test-only red first commit then minimal green implementation → runtime exact-head checks, independent PASS, verifier readback and governed merge → separate verified post-runtime permanent-main RAP rebind if provenance checks require → confirm exact-revision ordinary frontend deployment READY (no Production probe required: no user-visible surface) → source-first technical closeout with independent exact-head gates → final separate verified permanent-main RAP rebind. Every PR uses the runbook; one PR in merge step at a time; no protection bypass. Author/coordinator never attest. Verifier remains READ_ONLY except exactly one runbook `Hedgr-Verifier: PASS|FAIL sha=<40hex> run=<url>` comment, expressly permitted by its brief, after immediately re-reading live head and aborting on mismatch. Any new head requires fresh independent review. Close only this nested ticket; both parents remain open and research remains unreleased.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop for a needed string/figure not authorised by this brief, need for storage/clock/display metadata as computation inputs, doctrine/ADR change, genuine current-authority conflict, out-of-allowlist file, explicit hold, concrete gate blocker or Verifier FAIL. Source expansion requires a governed source amendment, merge and separate verified rebind before runtime continuation. Rollback: single revert of the runtime PR; authority rollback is a separate governed source/rebind sequence. Preserve the main checkout and unrelated worktrees/artifacts. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

Cross-reference: numbered §350; explicit §347 amendment is recorded there, not in the locked pack.

### Completed nested Lane V — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-STABILITY-REVIEW-MEMORY-001` [Closed §349 via #790 and #791]

**Authority / start:** Direct Founder approval, 6 Oct 2026 (AWST): ACCEPT the corrected inventory as evidence; crash fix first, then the Daniel slice brief. This bounded assignment authorises the full governed crash-fix sequence, ordinary automatic frontend Production deployments and the bounded Production probe, without routine repeat permission. Source authority becomes effective only on permanent-main merge and a separate independently verified projection-only RAP rebind before runtime. Lane E parent `SE-REASON-001` remains open without a nested ticket; G deferred.

**Objective:** Prevent an invalid saved review date from throwing during default Home review rendering. Validate saved review-memory entries when read. Invalid, missing or corrupt entries are discarded or ignored as no prior review entry, never rendered and never used to produce a plausible prior changed/unchanged memory row. The separate existing fingerprint comparison and valid visit-generated rows retain their behaviour.

**Exact file allowlist:**
- Source activation and later closeout: `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` (live §7/§7a and numbered records), `AGENTS.md` (live echo only), `apps/bridge-worker/tests/fork1-authority.test.mjs` (exact sequencing fixture only), and separately committed generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` after the source commit. No locked spec-pack edit or other fixture change.
- Runtime: `apps/frontend/lib/engine/review-snapshot-memory.ts` only, reusing the existing entry guard at the read boundary; no new abstraction or dependency.
- Runtime tests: `apps/frontend/__tests__/review-snapshot-memory.test.ts`, `apps/frontend/__tests__/engine-stability-review-snapshot.test.tsx`, and `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/review-memory-storage.spec.ts` (new hermetic default-Home regression under existing config).
- Each separate permanent-main RAP rebind: `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` only.
- Evidence outside tracked source: `/Users/musalwa/Projects/hedgr-copilot/output/home-review-memory-crash-20261006/`.

**Named red-to-green Acceptance Criteria (§346; no waiver):**
1. `review-snapshot-memory.test.ts`: `ignores an invalid saved date`, `treats missing memory as no prior review`, and `ignores malformed saved entries`; rejected entries do not enter the returned list. A mixed valid/rejected list preserves valid behaviour, stored order and the existing two-entry cap.
2. `engine-stability-review-snapshot.test.tsx`: `renders the Home review with an invalid saved date without rejected history` exercises the actual default-Home review component and reproduces `RangeError: Invalid time value` before the fix; companion missing/malformed cases render no rejected prior memory row or its changed/unchanged text. A valid-entry control remains visible.
3. `review-memory-storage.spec.ts`: hermetic `/dashboard` invalid-date, missing and malformed-storage cases reach and open the actual review section without a page error or rejected history/change signal; a valid saved-entry control renders normally. Account for the existing prior-fingerprint visit append: any fresh row is valid current-visit data, not rejected history.
4. FIRST RUNTIME COMMIT contains only reproducing tests, with retained red command/output on unchanged runtime. Do not edit runtime until that commit exists. Later fix commit makes them green; keep both commits visible for independent review (normal final squash merge allowed).
5. Focused memory, fingerprint and Home regressions plus local `pnpm run validate` and frontend `e2e:ci`; hosted `validate` and `E2E smoke (@hedgr/frontend)` on exact current head; distinct independent GPT-6.1 Sol high Verifier PASS before each PR merges. Author/coordinator never attest. Verifier is READ_ONLY except one exact runbook attestation, re-reading live head immediately before posting and aborting on mismatch. Read back successful `hedgr/verifier`; any head change requires fresh review. Owner-account update if behind main; no bypass; one PR in merge step at a time.

**Exclusions:** No Engine logic, EngineState, posture, notice text, copy or routes. No visit-on-every-load, journey-reset persistence or notice-text-fingerprint change; no other inventory drift item (including item 4), storage/clock architecture, new dependency, locked pack/§347 amendment, Daniel activation/implementation/brief, Lane E/G work, research/participant release, telemetry, provider/Worker configuration, financial capability or cross-lane authority.

**Sequence / closeout gates:** Source commit → separate deterministic branch RAP commit → validate, draft PR, independent exact-head review and governed source merge → separate verified permanent-main projection-only RAP rebind → separate runtime branch from effective current main, test-only red first commit then green fix → draft/runtime exact-head gates and governed merge → requested separate verified post-runtime permanent-main RAP rebind → exact-revision READY frontend Production deployment → bounded probe on `https://hedgr-copilot-frontend.vercel.app/dashboard` in hermetic isolated browser state: invalid date, missing and malformed memory must not crash or render rejected history/change signals; retain a valid control → source-first technical closeout, reconcile occupancy, exact-head review/merge → final separate verified permanent-main RAP rebind. Close only this nested ticket; keep both parents open and research route unreleased. Production configuration is unchanged.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop for genuine authority conflict, explicit hold, concrete gate blocker, needed out-of-allowlist file or material architecture/trust decision. A finding needing source expansion requires source amendment, merge and rebind before runtime continuation. Rollback: one revert of the runtime PR; authority rollback is a separate governed source/rebind sequence. ### Completed nested Lane V — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-STABILITY-LEDGER-ONLY-001` [Closed §345, 5 Oct 2026]

**Authority / start:** On 5 Oct 2026 (AWST) the Founder (Musalwa Hibajene) said “lets also fix the wallet vs ledgr configuration. I would recommend retiring the wallet configuration from production paths.” Later in the same session, answering the steward proposal that asked for approval of the scope, both §328 supersessions and the full sequence, the Founder said “approved to proceed”. This activates **only** nested `CLASS-A-VAL-002-STABILITY-LEDGER-ONLY-001` under open parent `CLASS-A-VAL-002`. Class A, synthetic. Foundation layer: **Stability** (`docs/strategy/PHASE-SEQUENCING-2026-10-05.md`); it respects the dissemination hold. No D-number. Source authority must merge to permanent main and receive a separate verified projection-only RAP rebind before any runtime. The research route remains unreleased. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

**Objective:** Retire wallet balance mode so ledger is the only balance source. `computeBalanceFromLedger` over the existing ledger store becomes the sole balance path on every route; the `'wallet'` mode, the `NEXT_PUBLIC_BALANCE_FROM_LEDGER` flag and the wallet store's credit/debit writes are removed. Ledger behaviour stays identical.

**Why:** Production has run ledger mode since §341 (82/82). The wallet branch is dead code on every built path, and the configuration split (Production wallet mode until §341; CI ledger only) is what let the §327 T3/T5 defect reach Production. Removing it retires that whole drift class, the kind of drift item the Stability pack asks to inventory (`docs/strategy/stability-engine-pack.md`, “Inventory what currently breaks or drifts”).

**Founder-approved supersessions (§328):**
1. §328 Decision 1 kept the flag as “a documented rollback”. Superseded: rollback is a **single revert** of the runtime PR, which meets AGENTS §4 (“flag or single revert”).
2. §328 retained the #732 wallet-mode unit tests. Superseded: they are removed with the wallet branch.

**Exact allowed files:**
- **Authority (this source PR):** `AGENTS.md` (notice); `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md` (§7, §7a, §328 / §341 supersession notes, §344); `apps/bridge-worker/tests/fork1-authority.test.mjs` (sequencing fixture only); generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json`.
- **Runtime (later PR only; not this source):**
  1. `apps/frontend/lib/state/balance.mode.ts`: retire `'wallet'` and the flag. Either delete the module and its call sites or reduce it to a ledger-only constant; the runtime PR picks the smaller reviewable diff and states why.
  2. `apps/frontend/lib/hooks/useBalance.ts`: ledger-only; remove the wallet fallback and the wallet store subscription.
  3. `apps/frontend/app/(app)/deposit/page.tsx` and `apps/frontend/app/(app)/withdraw/page.tsx`: remove wallet credit/debit and the `hedgr:wallet` writes; ledger behaviour identical.
  4. `apps/frontend/lib/deposits/synthetic-deposit-lifecycle.ts`: remove the wallet credit on settle.
  5. `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx`: remove `resetWallet` and the guards that exist only for wallet mode (the `getBalanceMode() === "ledger"` condition, keeping its ledger branch; the T3 “either balance mode” wording). On journey reset, remove any stale `hedgr:wallet` localStorage key.
  6. `apps/frontend/lib/state/wallet.ts`: delete if no consumer remains.
  7. `apps/frontend/config/env.ts`: drop the flag pass-through.
  8. Comments/docs in `apps/frontend/lib/state/balance.ts` and `useBalance.ts`.
  9. Tests under `apps/frontend/__tests__/` and `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/`: remove the #732 wallet-mode unit tests and tests that exist only for the removed wallet branch or store; update tests that reference wallet mode or seed `hedgr:wallet` only where needed; add one permanent hermetic Playwright regression under the existing config: a settled $5 ledger deposit plus a stale `hedgr:wallet` `usdBalance` of 7 shows `$5.00` on Home.
  10. `docs/ops/product-finish/HOME_EXPERIENCE_QA.md`: a short appended QA/evidence note.

**Must not change:** ledger maths (`computeBalanceFromLedger`); the ledger storage key or format; routes; participant-facing copy; Engine / `EngineState`; the research route; workflows; other environment flags; doctrine, ADRs or `.cursorrules`. Any other file is out of scope: stop and report.

**Known residuals outside scope (recorded, not edited):** `env/templates/frontend.env.schema` still lists the flag with its rollback comment, and `docs/ops/mobile-native/HEDGR_PLATFORM_BOUNDARY_REGISTER.md` lists the wallet store. After runtime the variable has no effect. Historical STATUS, AGENTS and QA records stay as written.

**Not this ticket:** Vercel settings (the Founder checks that Preview has no `NEXT_PUBLIC_BALANCE_FROM_LEDGER=false`; the app ignores the variable after runtime). Engine or Daniel-slice work. Same-session legibility observations (the $500.00 chart label; the duplicated “No change” pill). Participant release, Lane E/G, Green delegation, financial capability or cross-lane authority.

**Verification:** Runtime: `pnpm run validate` and `pnpm --filter @hedgr/frontend run e2e:ci` locally; hosted `validate` and `E2E smoke (@hedgr/frontend)` green; an independent Verifier PASS on the exact head checking the allowlist, no ledger-maths or copy change, the regression present and passing, and no remaining `getBalanceMode`, `useWalletStore` or `NEXT_PUBLIC_BALANCE_FROM_LEDGER` reference in `apps/frontend`. After merge and a READY Production deployment, the steward re-runs the §327 probe (ledger $5, stale wallet $7) on `https://hedgr-copilot-frontend.vercel.app` and expects `$5.00` on Home by construction.

**Stop / rollback:** Stop if any other file is needed; if ledger maths, the ledger storage key or format, copy, routes or Engine would change; if a Verifier finding needs a source change (amendment → merge → rebind first); on any authority conflict. Rollback: single revert of the runtime PR (restores the flag and wallet branch); revert this source and, after its rebind, that rebind. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

**Sequence:**
1. This source PR (§7, §7a, §344, supersession notes, AGENTS, fixture, branch RAP).
2. Separate permanent-main RAP rebind.
3. Runtime PR.
4. Production probe.
5. Source-first closeout and final rebind.

Cross-reference: numbered §344.

**Verified technical completion / effect:** §345 records #779 (`6b848e4` from verified head `6adc1b6`), merged after an independent Verifier PASS WITH NOTES on its exact head, with Production deployment `6863008500` READY and the §327 probe 82/82 on the public alias. On permanent-main merge of this source completion and a final separate verified RAP rebind, close only this nested ticket. No nested successor is named. Known residuals (`env/templates/frontend.env.schema`, `HEDGR_PLATFORM_BOUNDARY_REGISTER.md`) stay as recorded and are not reopened by this closeout. The research route remains unreleased. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

Sarah's page uses it. The later Mulenga ticket may reuse it; its frame rule “re-declare the chrome, don't extract it” is to be amended in that ticket's own source record.

### Completed nested Lane V — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-HOME-EXPERIENCE-001` (effective only after §327 source merge and separate RAP rebind)

**Authority / start:** Direct Founder approval on 2026-09-27: “We can activate NEW-MARKER after the proposed ticket. **Objective:** One Home experience on both routes. (2) Apply the approved productisation polish to both routes: since you were last here; one named next step after every action; first-use, loading, rate-unavailable and failed-confirmation states; one restrained motion. The balance keeps its single type size. The real-money concept is parked and excluded. Accepted references, decisions and copy: [HOME_EXPERIENCE_BASELINE.md](product-finish/HOME_EXPERIENCE_BASELINE.md).

**Founder decisions (accepted):** (1) The default simulated environment uses the journey balance wording; live mode keeps “Your current position” with no caption and receives no new money wording. (2) Keep the existing “…provides context, not an instruction.” sentence verbatim as one quiet line under “Your position” on default Home, preserving D-130 mobile visibility. (3) Keep “Simulate a withdrawal” as a third quiet text action on default Home. (4) Keep Recent activity and the first-time / “Nothing needs action” empty states, restyled; leave the `earn` “Return rate (context)” tile unchanged pending a separate decision. (5) Use the underlined current tab on every screen of both routes, with header navigation from 64rem. (6) Tranche 2–5 wording applies only in simulated contexts. (7) Add one local value: the last Home visit time in `localStorage`, cleared by journey reset and “Start again”; no server, telemetry or personal data. The position line is derived from existing ledger entries. (8) The Activity entry thread supersedes the 2026-09-19 peach balance-after strip. (9) One ticket in five tranches, each with its own PR, distinct QA and independent rollback.

**Exact copy:** The Founder-locked copy register in the baseline is exhaustive for new or changed strings. Every other approved string stays character-for-character, including “display estimate”, the four existing market-data continuity lines and “Retry rate”. Values derive from recorded state and the selected display currency; the reference amounts are examples, not runtime constants.

**Tranches:** **T1** translates the canonical Home to `/dashboard` (presentation only). **T2** covers Deposit and Withdraw on both routes: ReceiptCard, ActionDock, PillButton, the journey-only step thread, the restyled rate-unavailable panel (removing its raw `text-amber-950`), an inline failed-confirmation message that keeps the amount, and a Hedgr loading treatment replacing the `border-blue-600` spinner. **T3** covers Home on both routes in simulated contexts: the change chip, three Observation variants, the position line with its last-visit marker, the first-use card, the loading skeleton, the date line, and the last-visit value with its reset. **T4** is the Activity entry thread on both routes. **T5** is motion and depends on T3: a 600ms ease-out count and line settle only after a confirmed change, a 140ms chip fade, full suppression under reduced motion, and one screen-reader sentence.

**Exact allowed files (edit only as needed):**
- Authority/design: `AGENTS.md`, `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md`, generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json`, `DESIGN.md`.
- Accepted reference/evidence: `docs/ops/product-finish/HOME_EXPERIENCE_BASELINE.md`, `docs/ops/product-finish/home-experience/*.png` (13 references), `docs/ops/product-finish/home-experience-qa/*.png` (Founder disposition 2026-09-28 10:15 AWST: QA evidence screenshots supporting `HOME_EXPERIENCE_QA.md`; surfaced by #724 verifier FAIL), new `docs/ops/product-finish/HOME_EXPERIENCE_QA.md`.
- Runtime: `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx`, `synthetic-home.module.css`, `EnginePostureHeader.tsx`, `CurrencyInsight.tsx`, `currency-insight.module.css`; `apps/frontend/app/(app)/deposit/page.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/(app)/withdraw/page.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/(app)/activity/page.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/(app)/layout.client.tsx`; `apps/frontend/app/(app)/product-finish.module.css`, `research-wallet.module.css`, `shared-baseline.module.css`; `apps/frontend/components/MarketDataContinuityPanel.tsx`; new `apps/frontend/app/(app)/dashboard/PositionLine.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/(app)/ReceiptCard.tsx`, `apps/frontend/app/(app)/ActionDock.tsx`, `apps/frontend/lib/state/last-visit.ts`.
- Activation verification: `apps/bridge-worker/tests/fork1-authority.test.mjs`, solely for the exact current sequencing projection fixture.
- Verification: `apps/frontend/__tests__/dashboard.page.test.tsx`, `engine-posture-header.test.tsx`, `currency-insight.test.tsx`, `deposit.page.test.tsx`, `withdraw.page.test.tsx`, `activity.page.test.tsx`, `synthetic-deposit-lifecycle.test.ts`, `market-data-continuity-copy.test.ts`, new `last-visit.test.ts`; `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/product-finish.spec.ts`, `scope-first.spec.ts`, `shared-baseline.spec.ts`, `wallet-redesign.spec.ts`, `display-currency.spec.ts`, `currency-insight.spec.ts`, `class-a-val-002.spec.ts`, `route-hygiene.spec.ts`, `balance-ssot.spec.ts`, `product-route-convergence.spec.ts`, `smoke-pack.spec.ts`; and `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/stability-scenarios.spec.ts` solely for T3’s one superseded assertion after the research bridge into `/dashboard-synthetic-journey` (“Start with a simulated deposit” → the approved first-use copy) (Founder disposition 2026-09-28: T3 replaces that string per the copy register; no other line in that file, no research runtime, and no NEW-MARKER-001 scope). Change only expectations this brief supersedes; keep route, amount, arithmetic, mode, disclosure, eligibility and reset assertions strict.
- Nothing under `apps/frontend/app/research/**`, no `@hedgr/ui` package change, and no other file, token value, dependency, asset or route.

**Isolation / financial boundary:** No change to ledger, wallet, balance, available/pending or transaction-lifecycle logic, rates/FX, display-currency logic, providers, schema, server storage, engine computation/types/posture, target semantics, policy or feature flags, the `earn` tile’s behaviour, About/frozen narrative, Form/protocol, participant exposure or telemetry. The only new state is the local last-visit value (decision 7). Live mode gains no money wording or capability. Journey eligibility logic is unchanged. Nothing here touches `/research/stability-scenarios` or the held NEW-MARKER-001 files.

**Acceptance / completion:** Per tranche: full validate, production build, the complete hermetic browser suite, required hosted checks, and distinct governance and runtime QA. Inspect both routes in the journey, the default simulated environment and live mode, first-time and returning, at 320/390px and 1280/1440px, with 200% root text. ### Completed nested Lane V — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-RESEARCH-NEW-MARKER-001` (effective only after §330 source merge and separate RAP rebind; runtime #710 retained)

**Authority / start:** Direct Founder approval on 2026-09-25. This is the sole nested Lane V successor under open parent `CLASS-A-VAL-002`, Class A synthetic/informational UI presentation only. This source PR records authority only; source authority must merge to permanent main and receive a separate verified projection-only RAP rebind before runtime edits. The route remains unreleased. No D-number is assigned or reserved.

**Objective:** Change only the visual presentation of the existing `New` markers so each is a small, secondary UI status associated with the changed row heading in the After state. `New` means the row changed; it is not participant-facing prose or part of a value/sentence.

**Preserve exactly:** Before and After retain the same four rows, in the same order: Available now / Planned / Due / What to watch. Keep all locked participant-facing copy and underlying values character-for-character unchanged. Only After / Due and After / What to watch receive `New`. Preserve the Before/After structure, Sarah scenario, shortened fee-change screen, “Nothing else has changed.”, plain Continue action, research label, first-screen safeguard, attribution, limits, final research screen, navigation and research logic.

**Acceptance:** (1) `New` is visually associated with the After `Due` row heading and separated from its value. (2) `New` is visually associated with the After `What to watch` row heading and separated from its prose. (3) No marker appears on either Before row set or After Available now/Planned. (4) Copy, values, row order, route and scenario logic are unchanged. (5) Mobile is legible without clipping/overlap/confusing wrapping; desktop comparison alignment is preserved. (6) Before/After and marker semantics are covered by existing relevant automated tests, updated only as required. (7) Local screenshots or equivalent captured evidence cover mobile 390px and desktop 1440px, including Before and After states.

**Exact eligible files:** Source transition: `AGENTS.md`, `docs/ops/HEDGR_STATUS.md`, `apps/bridge-worker/tests/fork1-authority.test.mjs` only for the exact current sequencing projection fixture, and generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` only for the source-branch RAP required by checks and later separate permanent-main rebind. After activation/rebind: runtime `apps/frontend/app/research/stability-scenarios/ScenarioStimulus.tsx` only; verification `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/stability-scenarios.spec.ts` only if an assertion needs adjustment. Store requested visual evidence in a ticket-specific local output folder; do not add it to participant materials or the repo source PR. No other file without a new source amendment.

**Exclusions / stop:** No copy edits, explanation text, row/panel redesign, animation, broad reusable component migration, other screen/route, navigation, logic, scenario value, financial behavior, Engine/Wallet/ledger, safeguards, participant contact/release, response capture, Form, telemetry, personal input, D1/D2, Lane E/G, Green delegation or cross-lane authority. Stop if any acceptance point would require changing locked copy, another surface, participant exposure, or deployment/release. Roll back only this marker treatment and its scoped test assertion, then follow source-first closeout and separate RAP rebind. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

**Founder amendment — stacked rows (2026-09-27):** A distinct Codex Verifier reviewed unmerged local runtime commits `8639141` (Implementer) and `cf2d9e0` (verification) and returned PASS WITH NOTES. Its measurements at 1024/1280/1440px showed After `Due` rendering `Due` →8px→ `New` →12px→ value on one line, with the marker starting where the Before `Due` value starts (12px after its label). The Founder judged that `Due` and `New` sharing a line with the value risks a mistaken reading and approved stacking on every row. Runtime (same eligible files): in both Before and After, every row places its label (and any marker) above its value at all widths, matching the existing mobile and `What to watch` layout; the marker stays inside the row heading. This supersedes the `row/panel redesign` exclusion above only for label-over-value stacking; no other layout, spacing system, component or copy change is permitted. Additional acceptance: (8) at 390, 640, 1024, 1280 and 1440px and at 320px with 200% text, no row value shares a line with its row heading or marker in either state; (9) Before/After rows at desktop keep shared row tracks with top edges within 4px, Before stays first in the DOM, the rule stays above `What to watch` only, and no horizontal overflow occurs at 320/390/1280/1440px or 200% text. The existing 1280×800 desktop glance check continues to record any `What to watch` overflow as a WATCH, not a failure. Source merge to permanent main and a separate verified projection-only RAP rebind precede the runtime edit; the runtime change then lands as a new Implementer commit followed by a separate verification commit on the same draft runtime PR. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

### Completed nested Lane V — `CLASS-A-VAL-002-RESEARCH-COMPRESSION-001` (effective only after §314 source merge and separate RAP rebind)

**Authority / start:** On 2026-09-25 at ~14:59 AWST, Founder Musalwa Hibajene approved this bounded implementation ticket as the sole nested Lane V occupancy under open parent `CLASS-A-VAL-002`, with delegated carry-through to Barry (Repo Steward) for this ticket only (“Happy for you to carry it through all the way until it lands on main”; at ~15:00 AWST, “as long as you followed established governance, you are good to go.”). Copy source is Founder-locked PE-RESEARCH-COMPANION-PASS-001 copy v3 (~14:57 AWST). Predecessor `CLASS-A-VAL-002-RESEARCH-VALUE-PANEL-001` is closed (§311/§312, #678–#682, main `e6d9797e78e595270f5276cee1d310072a9fbd17`). Class A synthetic/informational. Green in consequence; classification creates no delegation. Source authority must merge to permanent main and receive a separately verified projection-only RAP rebind before any runtime edit. This source PR records authority only and must not implement UI. Exact accepted participant strings for every changed slot are recorded in this brief so the runtime Implementer does not need off-repo files. No D-number is assigned or reserved. Registering Human Narrative Lead (§9.9) and Product Experience Lead (§9.8) is identity only and does not assign Sue or Kip a task in this ticket. D2 is a separate, undecided Founder strategy question and is not activated or recorded as work. [Superseded §343, 5 Oct 2026: decided option B — hypothesis only; not product direction.]

**Launch-approval and delegation record (frame §0; governs):** Approving this ticket does not approve any launch. Each of S1, S2, S3, S4 Verifier, S5 and S6 needs its own explicit Founder launch approval at the time it is needed. For THIS ticket only, those launch approvals are granted in advance as a standing approval, exercised by Barry (Repo Steward) one step at a time. No agent may launch, or chain-launch, the next step’s resource on its own. Stop conditions still return to the Founder. Wherever a later step says “Founder merges”, read “the Founder, or Barry exercising the Founder’s recorded delegation for this ticket, merges through ordinary protections and required checks”.

**S3 auto-merge-OFF gate:** Auto-merge stays OFF on every PR of this ticket. The S3 runtime PR must never have auto-merge enabled before the S4 Verifier report is back. Keep that PR in draft (recommended) until then. S3 merges only after the S4 Verifier report is back and PASS (or PASS WITH NOTES with no material finding). If auto-merge is found enabled on the S3 PR before the S4 report, disable it and escalate; do not merge.

**Objective:** Revise only the unreleased `/research/stability-scenarios` path so that it renders copy v3 exactly. This is subtraction and hierarchy only. Everything else on the path is inherited unchanged from the verified #680 presentation (`7d9b521a9a0e03a4beba1834047f92fef4bd70cb`). Continue-only. The route remains unreleased research.

**Claim fences:** No FX prediction (direction, size or range); no funded/coverage verdict or on-screen arithmetic; not reading reviewer money; not Engine output; not advice; no ontology chrome. No Hedgr or `we` subject in any row value. No present-tense Hedgr capability (`Hedgr shows`, `Hedgr tracks`) anywhere on the route. No `position` / `judgement` / `interprets` in participant copy. **No K30,000 or any total (D1). No D2 wording. No benefit line.** No column-of-figures styling.

**Copy source of truth (on-repo):** Render with typographic `’` (U+2019) and `·` (U+00B7) where shown. Token mapping: `{amt6000}` → `amount('6,000')`; `{amt24000}` → `amount('24,000')`; `{amt29500}` → `amount('29,500')`; `{localName}` → `savingsCurrencyName`. For ZMW, `amount()` renders `K{v}` and `{localName}` is `kwacha`, except the A2 fee-change line, which keeps the existing `Zambian kwacha` render. Five selected currencies remain ZMW/KES/NGN/GHS/PHP. One selected denomination is held through a traversal.

**Gate-2 micro-render resolutions (Founder, 2026-09-25; pin so the Implementer invents nothing):** (a) Row labels render WITHOUT a trailing colon (`Available now`, `Planned`, `Due`, `What to watch`), per copy v3 literal. (b) The marker is the text `New` inside the same `dd`, after the value. Colour may reinforce it but never replaces it. (c) Kip §4 check 8 is superseded by v3: A2 must show `Nothing else has changed.`; `All other facts remain the same.` is a retired string.

**Common boundary (header, every stage; keep `data-testid="study-common-boundary"`):** REPLACE `Only the information shown here is being considered. No response is entered or saved on this page.` with `This example uses only the facts on this page. Nothing you do here is saved.` It is one JSX element in the shared header, so the swap applies to A1, A2, B and Bridge at once and stays identical across a traversal.

**Boundary-string scope finding (frame §6.3; DONE, no widening):** Runtime occurrence of the old string is only `apps/frontend/app/research/stability-scenarios/ScenarioStimulus.tsx` (`data-testid="study-common-boundary"`). It is not a shared component and not in orientation. Tests: only `apps/frontend/tests-e2e/stability-scenarios.spec.ts` (Test 1 exact text; Test 2 identity-across-stages survives). Docs (no edit): (a) `docs/ops/governance/mvp/HEDGR_STABILITY_INTERPRETATION_CONVERGENCE_PACKAGE.md` §6 lists the old string as a completed §301 historical *candidate* reminder (“may say”). Deliberate runtime supersession: do not treat that package as live chrome; the Verifier must not flag drift against it. The §6/§17 within-traversal chrome-identity rule remains met because the one header string is identical on every stage. (b) This file’s completed §311 brief quotes the old string as history and is not edited. Result: no scope widening. One runtime file and the one spec already in scope. No Founder scope decision is needed.

**A1 (stage `a1`):** All A1 strings other than the common boundary remain inherited unchanged, including `Situation on 1 October 2026`, the facts paragraph, the A1 fee line, h2 `Sarah is saving for a postgraduate course.`, and Continue `Continue to the changed course fee`.

**A2 (stage `a2`) — order top → bottom after inherited chrome/header:**
1. h2 `Sarah’s changed course fee` (KEEP)
2. `The course provider has changed the fee to {amt29500}, payable in {localName} on 1 October 2027. It was USD 1,000, payable in US dollars.` (ZMW remains `Zambian kwacha`)

**Layout / placement (Kip `PE-RESEARCH-COMPANION-PASS-001-kip.md` §3, summarised):** One panel in Beat B; both states visible with no interaction. Mobile (390px) and 200% text: stacked Before then After, same row order; width-based switching that also reflows at 200% text. Each state is a labelled `section` with one `dl`. Focus on stage entry moves to the Beat title h2. Before comes first in the DOM at every width.

**200% reflow note (frame §6.4):** the existing 200% test sets the root `font-size` to 200% at 320px. Tailwind v4 (`tailwindcss ^4.1.11`) breakpoints are media queries, which do not respond to the root CSS font-size. Kip §4 check 5 (a 200% grid reflows to stacked) therefore needs a mechanism that does respond to it (e.g. container-based switching), or an explicitly specified test method. Confirm this in S3, and stop if it needs files outside `ScenarioStimulus.tsx`. No Tailwind config, global CSS or shared-component edits.

**Existing baseline and effective gate:** The existing Cloudflare One organisation has team domain `orange-moon-cc9e.cloudflareaccess.com` in account `0c2e1b39c4e7fc367c74e6d8d1792991`. The same account's Worker dashboard shows the existing `hedgrops-bridge` Production service and `https://hedgrops-bridge.hedgr.workers.dev/` visit link; no protected endpoint was called. The observed Worker has no connected production bindings. This is an observed account baseline, not acceptance of a deployed MCP path. The Founder grants only the policy/application permission below, effective after this source record merges and the separate deterministic projection-only RAP rebind is verified on actual permanent-main history. Until then, save neither Cloudflare object. No replacement ticket, Lane R occupancy or D-number is created.

**Repository recording:** Eligible files are `AGENTS.md`, this status file and generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` only. Commit source first. Generate the branch RAP from the explicit committed source revision with the existing tool and commit it separately; preserve `MAIN_HISTORY_UNVERIFIED` and all non-authorising flags. Run focused authority/RAP fixtures and full required validation. Use ordinary PR review, labels, required hosted checks and native GitHub rules; do not bypass or auto-merge. Recheck direct remote main and preserve intervening work. Source merge and separate permanent-main projection-only RAP rebind may trigger only the identified `hedgr-copilot-frontend` Vercel Production deployments. Verify each revision and non-mutating smoke checks. Before merge record the last verified production deployment for bounded Vercel rollback; if the automation would also deploy the Worker or mutate Cloudflare configuration, stop. A regression is contained through the recorded Vercel deployment and a scoped repo revert with its own separate RAP rebind, preserving unrelated commits.

**Owner-only Cloudflare sequence after the §316 main rebind:** The single `Founder only` Allow policy with one `Include → Emails` entry was saved before the §315 gate became effective; this is a recorded sequence deviation, not retrospectively compliant. The Founder subsequently confirmed its sole approved sign-in identity in the owner-controlled Cloudflare interface. Do not infer identity from dashboard account name or chat history, add another broad Include/Allow/Bypass rule, or create a duplicate policy. At the §316 checkpoint, the saved policy inherited the application session setting and was not yet associated with an application. After the §316 source merge #690 and separate verified permanent-main RAP rebind #693, the owner associated the policy and saved the Access for SaaS OIDC application. Confirm that the existing Worker host still belongs to this account. Associate the saved policy only with the `HedgrOps Bridge` Access for SaaS OIDC application. Its Cloudflare-side redirect is exactly `https://hedgrops-bridge.hedgr.workers.dev/callback`, distinct from `/mcp` and the later ChatGPT OAuth redirect. Before the owner saves the application, inspect the association, callback, selected login method, effective policy and exposed OIDC token settings; retain the observed five-minute OIDC access-token lifetime without calling it an application-session duration. Record any unexposed application-session setting as unresolved. After the owner saves, inspect the saved configuration and resolve the applicable session and reauthentication behaviour before approving live Bridge use. Do not change another application, policy or shared identity provider; stop if that becomes necessary. Retain the issued Access client secret securely under owner control; do not transmit it through chat, repository files, screenshots, logs or plugin configuration.

**Hold at the identity checkpoint (superseded only by effective §317):** This did not establish or change `MCP_ALLOWED_SUBJECT`; enter no `ACCESS_CLIENT_SECRET` or other Worker secret; create or bind no KV or Durable Object; change no Worker variable, code, migration or deployed version; call no protected Bridge/MCP endpoint; perform no end-to-end OAuth test or plugin registration/connection. No paid-plan upgrade, wider identity, tool, permission, product, research, participant, financial or cross-lane authority follows. §317 supplies the later bounded Worker diagnostic gate; plugin connection and normal-use acceptance require a separate decision. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

### Current bounded Worker diagnostic gate — `BRIDGE-MCP-001` (§317; no live Lane R occupancy)

**Effect and repository sequence:** The Founder retains the completed identity-configuration checkpoint: the existing `HedgrOps Bridge` Access for SaaS OIDC application has the saved, one-email `Founder only` Allow policy and exact Worker `/callback` redirect; the latest issued client secret remains privately held by the owner. No repeat application setup or secret disclosure is needed. This diagnostic permission supersedes the §315/§316 Worker configuration and protected-test holds **only after** this source record merges to permanent main and a separate projection-only RAP rebind verifies against the actual merged source revision. Eligible recording files are `AGENTS.md`, this status file and generated `docs/ops/bridge/repo-authority-projection.json` only. Commit source before generating a separately committed deterministic branch RAP from that exact commit; preserve `MAIN_HISTORY_UNVERIFIED` where applicable and all read-only/non-authorising flags. Run focused fixtures and full validation, ordinary PR review/required checks and source merge, then the separate main rebind. Recheck direct remote main, branch protections and final diff; preserve unrelated Product Experience work. Only the identified automatic `hedgr-copilot-frontend` Vercel Production deployments from these bounded recording merges are included. The current pre-merge Vercel rollback anchor is GitHub Production deployment `6662473587`, successful for `hedgr-copilot-frontend` at permanent-main `4e31918858fd6b46603b38387f6fd7e111e5dbf7`; recheck the latest verified production revision immediately before merge. Verify resulting revisions and non-mutating smoke checks. On a Vercel regression, contain through that verified deployment and a scoped source revert with its own separate RAP rebind, preserving unrelated commits. If another production side effect appears, stop. No force merge, bypass or workflow change.

**Exact Worker candidate and owner preconditions:** At adoption, direct remote main is `4e31918858fd6b46603b38387f6fd7e111e5dbf7`. Deploy only the existing `hedgrops-bridge` Worker implementation integrated by PR #660 at `47fe34a525a51bee50a22a84b49bc843933a8189`; its runtime and Wrangler configuration are unchanged on that main revision. Recheck the final main revision and Worker diff before deployment; any material code/config change requires affected review and a new disposition. Record the then-active Worker version as a separate Worker rollback anchor. The approved Founder sign-in email was owner-confirmed privately, but the exact Access user ID for `MCP_ALLOWED_SUBJECT` is **unverified**. Before enabling MCP, the owner establishes that value through owner-controlled Cloudflare identity records, never by email inference or an unverified decoded token; failure to establish it stops configuration. The owner also confirms the Workers plan and the request/storage limits and cost of one `OAUTH_KV` namespace plus the `OAUTH_SINGLE_USE` SQLite Durable Object binding/migration `bridge-mcp-oauth-single-use-v1`. Zero Trust seats do not establish Workers storage cost. A paid upgrade or commitment requires another decision.

**Owner-controlled configuration and deployment:** Only after the main-bound gate and owner preconditions, configure the existing Worker with `OAUTH_KV` and `OAUTH_SINGLE_USE`, the fixed `MCP_RESOURCE=https://hedgrops-bridge.hedgr.workers.dev/mcp`, the verified `MCP_ALLOWED_SUBJECT`, and the saved application's Access-issued `ACCESS_ISSUER`, `ACCESS_AUTHORIZATION_URL`, `ACCESS_TOKEN_URL`, `ACCESS_JWKS_URL` and `ACCESS_CLIENT_ID` checked against its OIDC discovery document. The owner enters the **latest retained** `ACCESS_CLIENT_SECRET` directly in Cloudflare Worker secrets or `wrangler secret put`, without sending or recording its value in chat, repository/plugin files, screenshots, logs or tests. Preserve existing `GITHUB_TOKEN` restricted to repository contents read and `HEDGROPS_BRIDGE_API_KEY` for legacy clients. The Cloudflare-side callback remains `https://hedgrops-bridge.hedgr.workers.dev/callback`; the later ChatGPT redirect is separate. Do not alter the Access policy, unrelated identity provider, tools, route mappings, legacy authentication, evidence contracts or repository permissions. Missing or invalid identity, binding, endpoint, secret or token validation fails closed; no API-key fallback on `/mcp`. Deploy only the pinned candidate to the existing service and record the deployed version.

**Single Founder-only diagnostic window:** After deployment, inspect public OAuth metadata and confirm unauthenticated `/mcp` rejection; verify legacy GET route/method/API-key behavior without changing legacy credentials. In an owner-controlled authenticated test, verify the Access ID token signature, issuer, exact client-ID audience, nonce, expiry and `sub` match before treating the Founder subject as confirmed. With a registered client using only the existing exact ChatGPT redirect allowlist, test the consent-bound authorization flow, four fixed no-argument evidence tools, source provenance, freshness/coverage/conflict qualifications, and deterministic unavailable/invalid responses. No plugin registration or connection is authorised. If a full live code exchange requires that connection, mark it **unverified** and return for a narrower connection-test gate; do not widen redirect rules, invent a bootstrap bypass or claim normal use. Record separately (1) Cloudflare sign-in/session and observed reauthentication behavior, (2) the **observed configured five-minute OIDC token lifetime**, and (3) Worker-issued 15-minute MCP bearer-token expiry, rejection and reauthorization, with no refresh grant. The saved SaaS UI does not expose a numeric application-session duration; inspect an authoritative saved setting if available and observe expiration when feasible. A short window does not establish a longer session's expiry. Never infer unlimited access or equate the OIDC setting with the downstream MCP session. Keep an unverified session question open for the next decision.

**Stop and migration-compatible recovery:** Stop on identity mismatch, unexpected access, replay, missing evidence qualifications, legacy regression, unavailable storage, configuration uncertainty or unapproved cost. Invoke the recorded migration-compatible recovery procedure while preserving Durable Object code-claim records and legacy Bridge availability; restore the predeployment Worker version **only where Cloudflare permits rollback across the applied migration**. Do not delete KV grants or the Durable Object while matching grants may remain readable. Preserve qualified evidence retrieval via legacy Bridge clients or GitHub fallback. Record diagnostic results before any separate plugin connection or normal-use acceptance. No participant, product, research, financial, ticket activation, repository mutation or cross-lane authority follows. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

**Connection sequence and acceptance:** A private, Founder-only ChatGPT MCP connection to `https://hedgrops-bridge.hedgr.workers.dev/mcp` may be registered after the main-bound §318 rebind and deployed-version/unauthenticated checks, solely to complete an otherwise unavailable live OAuth code exchange and four-tool diagnostic. Roll back via §317's migration-compatible recovery, preserving claim records and legacy service. No packaged skill/mandate edit or new OAuth server is authorised by this record. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

**Callback-failure evidence and bounded amendment (2026-09-27):** On active Worker version `b2be9c4c-a349-48e4-b3e6-41c2ed5ae5e4`, the Founder completed Cloudflare sign-in and approval for the expected registered ChatGPT client. The `OAUTH_SINGLE_USE` Durable Object returned success for atomic access-flow consumption; the Worker then returned a handled `503` from `/callback`. Invocation logging exposed no substage and tracing was disabled. Public discovery and JWKS responded successfully and matched the saved non-secret configuration, but those external checks do not prove the Worker's token fetch, JWKS fetch, provider client lookup or authorization completion succeeded. Identity equality, authorization-code issuance, MCP-token issuance, tool discovery/calls and session behavior remain unverified. The consumed flow is not reusable.

**Eligible remediation:** After this source and its separately committed branch RAP merge through ordinary review/checks and a separate projection-only permanent-main rebind verifies, change only `apps/bridge-worker/src/oauth.js` and `apps/bridge-worker/tests/oauth.test.mjs`. Emit only a fixed event name plus one fixed category from `access_token_fetch_unavailable`, `access_jwks_unavailable`, `provider_client_lookup_unavailable`, `provider_authorization_completion_unavailable`, or `callback_unexpected_failure`. Do not log exception objects/messages, URLs, query strings, request headers/bodies, authorization code/state, cookies, tokens, claims, subject/email/client metadata, secrets or upstream bodies. Preserve the public fail-closed response contract. Add focused tests for every category and assertions that representative sensitive values never enter logs. No dependency, binding, Durable Object migration, OAuth provider fork, credential, identity/policy, redirect, scope, token lifetime, legacy route or evidence-contract change is eligible.

**Delivery, retry and stop:** This source/RAP sequence includes its automatic `hedgr-copilot-frontend` Vercel Production deployments. Review the actual patch and tests, satisfy required checks, merge without bypass, and complete the separate permanent-main RAP rebind before implementation. Then deploy the exact reviewed Worker revision to the existing `hedgrops-bridge` service without changing bindings or secrets; record active version `b2be9c4c-a349-48e4-b3e6-41c2ed5ae5e4` as the migration-compatible rollback anchor. Inspect only the fixed category if it fails. Stop after that retry outcome; any substantive fix, tracing enablement, extra retry, configuration change or normal plugin use requires a new disposition. Rollback uses the recorded active version only where Cloudflare permits it after the existing SQLite migration, preserving claim records and legacy availability. **NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.**

### Finite non-lane governance/documentation — `OPS-GOV-STATUS-RESTRUCTURE-001` (§438; §6f.30; expires on closeout)

**Pre-execution baseline refresh (R0; Amendment 1, §439):** The Implementer fetches main and records `B_exec`, then diffs `11ffe8964856129d53bb420f90d847c2346fead5..B_exec` for AGENTS and STATUS. The effective activation rebind is `11ffe8964856129d53bb420f90d847c2346fead5`; it includes the §438 activation changes to AGENTS and STATUS. Their identity to pre-activation `11f5f42792e93d01420add9d10afaee6a63fa6ce` is not asserted; any later changes are covered by this comparison and reclassification.

- **A1 Meaning preserved:** every active, held, permitted, excluded, dependency and stop item is mapped to a current source holding its complete wording.
- **A1a Continuing terms byte-identical:** the §437 text, D1, the Home permission text and the LOCK COPY/term ban.

- **A10 Guard test:** the Q-S5 guard passes, and its negative fixture fails.

**Stops:** Stop and return to the Founder/HedgrOps on any of the following:
- the active, held or permitted set changed at `B_exec`;
- any classification needs an inferred supersession;
- a continuing rule would exist only in history;
- §7 can't fit within 6,500 B without dropping operative pointers;
- a generator, contract, workflow, runbook or other excluded-path change is needed;
- a RAP `payload.fields` difference;
- a byte mismatch in moved text;
- a SEC-DEPS-002 same-file conflict;
- a research-priority conflict;
- any authority conflict (AGENTS §13).

The target is never met by moving operative wording out.

**§364 rebind:** This changes RAP-mandatory STATUS and AGENTS, and §7/§7a text, so it is an authority-effect record. The §364(A) deferral does not apply. After source merge, a **separate** independently verified sole-JSON permanent-main §364 rebind PR follows, with its own hosted gates, and then a fetched-main `bridge:rap:check` PASS. The same applies to the closeout.

**Closeout:** A technical closeout record (next free §) cites the implementation PR and squash SHA, `B_exec`, the measured before/after reading figures, the A1–A10 results and the index location. It closes OPS-GOV-STATUS-RESTRUCTURE-001 and expires §6f.30 only after the closeout source merge, the separate sole-JSON §364 rebind and a fetched-main RAP PASS. No successor activates; the rebind redesign (Option 2) is a separate, later proposal.

**Rollback:** One governed source revert, with occupancy reconciliation and a separate §364 rebind, restores the pre-restructure AGENTS/STATUS. History files are removed by the same revert, and D1/D2 are reverted only by a new Founder record. NO CROSS-LANE IMPACT.
